# Chapter One Part A excerpt — access and opportunity

## English source passage

This subsection sets out what access and opportunity mean in practice:

- Sentients need practical paths to [Participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), education, work, care, safety, movement, and other goods that matter to ordinary life.
- Those paths must not be blocked, priced out, delayed, hidden, or tilted for arbitrary or irrelevant reasons.
- A door that is open only on paper is not enough where this Constitution requires **substantive** opportunity.

## Draft translation (id)

Subbagian ini menjelaskan arti akses dan kesempatan dalam praktik:

- Para sentien memerlukan jalur praktis menuju [Partisipasi](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), pendidikan, pekerjaan, perawatan, keselamatan, mobilitas, dan kebutuhan lain dalam kehidupan sehari-hari.
- Jalur tersebut tidak boleh dihalangi, dibuat tak terjangkau, ditunda, disembunyikan, atau diarahkan secara tidak adil karena alasan sewenang-wenang atau tidak relevan.
- Pintu yang hanya terbuka di atas kertas tidak cukup ketika Konstitusi ini mensyaratkan kesempatan yang **substantif**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.1 — Meaningful Agency and Consent

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

[Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) is the ability to make a choice between real options. [Consent](core_05.055_band_participation.md#consent) is a valid yes to one specific option. Consent does not count when the options are not distinct from each other or are not meaningful. A signed form alone creates neither meaningful agency nor consent. [Coercion and Manipulation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#coercion-and-manipulation) destroy both. The detailed rules are in Chapter Five §2 *Agency, consent, and anti-coercion*.

## Draft translation (id)

[Agensi yang bermakna](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) adalah kemampuan memilih di antara pilihan yang nyata. [Persetujuan](core_05.055_band_participation.md#consent) adalah jawaban ya yang sah terhadap satu pilihan tertentu. Persetujuan tidak berlaku jika pilihan-pilihannya tidak berbeda satu sama lain atau tidak bermakna. Formulir bertanda tangan saja tidak menciptakan agensi yang bermakna maupun persetujuan. [Paksaan dan manipulasi](core_05.055_band_participation.md#coercion-and-manipulation) merusak keduanya. Aturan terperinci terdapat di Bab Lima §2 *Agensi, persetujuan, dan anti-paksaan*.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A reader map, Flourishing and the four sustaining conditions; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — how the aims are developed

## English source

**Where Chapter One develops each aim.** Flourishing is wellbeing sustained through truth, safety, trustworthiness, and meaningful agency ([Preamble §1](core_00.000_preamble.md#flourishing)). Chapter One states the outcome first, then one principle for each of the four conditions.

## id draft

**Cara Bab Satu mengembangkan setiap arah.** Flourishing adalah kesejahteraan yang dipertahankan melalui kebenaran, keselamatan, keterpercayaan, dan agensi yang bermakna ([§1 Preambul](core_00.000_preamble.md#flourishing)). Bab Satu menyatakan hasilnya terlebih dahulu, lalu menetapkan satu prinsip untuk masing-masing dari empat kondisi tersebut.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aims_tetrad_two_axes.md bytes=1584 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A reader guidance, relationship between the Aims and Tetrad; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — the Aims and Tetrad as two axes

## English source

**Two axes, not a conflict.** The aims say what systems pursue; the Tetrad says how that pursuit stays legitimate. A term can sit on both: Truth and Trustworthiness are constituents of Flourishing, and [Preamble §2](core_00.000_preamble.md#2-measurements-overview) measures them under the Oversight family. [§§13–15](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) govern how these principles are weighed when they collide and read as one whole, and [§20](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#20-integrated-application) applies them to every later chapter.

## id draft

**Dua sumbu, bukan konflik.** Kedua Arah menjelaskan apa yang dikejar sistem; Tetrad menjelaskan bagaimana upaya itu tetap sah. Suatu istilah dapat berada pada keduanya: Truth dan Trustworthiness merupakan unsur Flourishing, dan [§2 Preambul](core_00.000_preamble.md#2-measurements-overview) mengukurnya dalam keluarga Pengawasan. [§§13–15](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) mengatur penimbangan prinsip-prinsip tersebut saat berbenturan dan cara membacanya sebagai satu kesatuan; [§20](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#20-integrated-application) menerapkannya pada setiap bab berikutnya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_recognition_pathways.md bytes=1494 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.5 Aligned Recognition Pathways; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — aligned recognition pathways

## English source passage

Named recognition pathways, acclaim, prizes, certification, standing, reputation effects, or comparable incentives that **allocate status**, **resources**, or **material access** must remain consistent with Truth, Safety, contestable procedure where **Chapter Six** assigns it, [Participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), and [Incentive Alignment](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment).

They **must not** systematically reward harm, deception, avoidance of scrutiny, extraction, or erosion of meaningful agency.

## Draft translation (id)

Jalur pengakuan yang ditetapkan, pujian, hadiah, sertifikasi, kedudukan, dampak reputasi, atau insentif sebanding yang **mengalokasikan status**, **sumber daya**, atau **akses material** harus konsisten dengan Kebenaran, Keselamatan, prosedur yang dapat digugat bila ditetapkan **Bab Enam**, [Partisipasi](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), dan [Penyelarasan Insentif](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment).

Semua itu **tidak boleh** secara sistematis memberi imbalan atas bahaya, penipuan, penghindaran pemeriksaan, ekstraksi, atau pengikisan agensi yang bermakna.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_no_approval.md bytes=1143 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Receipt Is Not Approval

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Being received, routed, or answered is not approval of the authors' conclusions. Any governing or merits effect requires the separate lawful authority, evidence, fair process, review, and remedy this Constitution assigns. The full no-self-appointment rule lives in [§17.4 Aligned Self-Organization](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization).

## Draft translation (id)

Diterima, disalurkan, atau dijawabnya pekerjaan bukan berarti kesimpulan penulis disetujui. Dampak apa pun terhadap tata kelola atau pokok perkara memerlukan kewenangan sah yang terpisah, bukti, proses yang adil, peninjauan, dan pemulihan sebagaimana ditetapkan Konstitusi ini. Aturan lengkap yang melarang pengangkatan diri sendiri menjadi pemegang kewenangan terdapat di [§17.4 Pengorganisasian Diri yang Selaras](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_no_power.md bytes=1382 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Procedural Path Does Not Grant Power

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

That path is not a grant of power. Starting, running, funding, publishing, or submitting the work does not, by itself:
- give anyone the power to govern others, enforce against them, or coerce them
- bind sentients who did not agree to a substantive outcome
- decide standing, liability, entitlement, validity, a mandate, a remedy, a classification, or a rights restriction
- count as a [Merits Determination](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) — a binding decision on the substance of the dispute

## Draft translation (id)

Jalur itu bukan pemberian kuasa. Memulai, menjalankan, mendanai, menerbitkan, atau mengajukan pekerjaan tersebut tidak dengan sendirinya:
- memberi siapa pun kuasa untuk memerintah orang lain, menegakkan tindakan terhadap mereka, atau memaksa mereka
- mengikat makhluk berkesadaran yang tidak menyetujui hasil substantif
- memutus kedudukan, tanggung jawab, hak, keabsahan, mandat, pemulihan, klasifikasi, atau pembatasan hak
- dianggap sebagai [Penetapan Pokok Perkara](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#merits-determination) — keputusan mengikat mengenai substansi sengketa

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_opening.md bytes=1610 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Aligned Self-Organization Opening

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you may start legitimate work without a sponsor, and institutions must give credible work a real procedural path — but that path is not power to govern others, and it is not a final decision on the substance.*

The freedom to assemble or create a system includes a real way to start work this Constitution treats as legitimate, without waiting for stewards already in charge to sponsor it. [§17.4 Aligned Self-Organization](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization) is the operative home. This subsection applies that rule at the Freedom / assembly-and-formation layer.

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Anda dapat memulai pekerjaan yang sah tanpa sponsor, dan lembaga harus menyediakan jalur prosedural nyata bagi pekerjaan yang kredibel — tetapi jalur itu bukan kewenangan untuk memerintah orang lain dan bukan keputusan akhir atas pokok perkara.*

Kebebasan untuk berkumpul atau menciptakan sistem mencakup cara nyata untuk memulai pekerjaan yang dianggap sah oleh Konstitusi ini, tanpa menunggu sponsor dari pengampu yang sudah menjabat. Tempat operatif aturan ini adalah [§17.4 Pengorganisasian Diri yang Selaras](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#174-aligned-self-organization). Subbagian ini menerapkan aturan itu pada tingkat Kebebasan, perkumpulan, dan pembentukan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__aligned_self_org_procedure.md bytes=866 -->
# Chapter One §7.2.1 — Procedural Path for Credible Work

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2.1 Aligned Self-Organization

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

When that work is credible and materially relevant, institutions must give it a real procedural path:
- receive it
- preserve it where warranted
- route it
- give a reasoned response
- have it reviewed by someone independent of those whose actions are being examined

## Draft translation (id)

Jika pekerjaan itu kredibel dan relevan secara material, lembaga harus menyediakan jalur prosedural yang nyata:
- menerimanya
- menyimpannya bila diperlukan
- menyalurkannya ke jalur yang tepat
- memberikan tanggapan beralasan
- meminta peninjauan oleh orang yang independen dari mereka yang tindakannya sedang diperiksa

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_design_conduct.md bytes=700 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Design and Conduct

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Design and conduct.** Processes must not be designed, framed, carried out, or allowed to operate as degradation, humiliation, spectacle, retaliation, discriminatory burdening, or convenience-driven rights erosion.

## Draft translation (id)

**Perancangan dan pelaksanaan.** Proses tidak boleh dirancang, dibingkai, dijalankan, ataupun dibiarkan beroperasi sebagai perendahan martabat, penghinaan, tontonan, pembalasan, pembebanan diskriminatif, atau pengikisan hak demi kemudahan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_plain_language.md bytes=1496 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Plain-Language Explanation

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: however you govern, enforce, judge, restrict, or remedy — you don't run sentients through humiliation, public spectacle, retaliation, or "because it's easier for us" cruelty. Fair consequences, public accountability, and firm restrictions can still be lawful even when they hurt or embarrass someone. What crosses the line is when the process itself is the punishment — designed to degrade, shame, or lash out rather than to protect, correct, restore, or prevent. This applies everywhere constitutional authority runs, not only during rights tradeoffs.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Sederhananya: apa pun cara Anda memerintah, menegakkan aturan, mengadili, membatasi, atau memberi pemulihan, jangan membuat sentien mengalami penghinaan, tontonan publik, pembalasan, atau kekejaman “karena itu lebih mudah bagi kami.” Konsekuensi yang adil, akuntabilitas publik, dan pembatasan tegas tetap dapat sah meskipun menyakiti atau mempermalukan seseorang. Batasnya dilanggar ketika proses itu sendiri menjadi hukuman—dirancang untuk merendahkan, mempermalukan, atau melampiaskan amarah, bukan untuk melindungi, mengoreksi, memulihkan, atau mencegah. Prinsip ini berlaku di mana pun kewenangan konstitusional dijalankan, bukan hanya saat menimbang hak.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_prohibited_conditions.md bytes=1148 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Prohibited Conditions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Anti-Degrading-Process Principle.** Constitutional processes, measures, and outcomes must satisfy this principle.

**Prohibited.** They must not be justified by, include, or predictably create:

- degrading treatment;
- humiliation for its own sake;
- spectacle used mainly to deter;
- retaliatory grievance;
- collective retaliation;
- discriminatory burdening; or
- procedural convenience overriding rights.

## Draft translation (id)

**Prinsip Proses Tanpa Degradasi.** Proses, tindakan, dan hasil konstitusional wajib mematuhi prinsip ini.

**Dilarang.** Semua itu tidak boleh dibenarkan oleh, mencakup, atau secara dapat diperkirakan menimbulkan:

- perlakuan yang merendahkan martabat;
- penghinaan demi penghinaan itu sendiri;
- tontonan yang terutama digunakan untuk mencegah;
- keluhan yang bersifat pembalasan;
- pembalasan kolektif;
- pembebanan diskriminatif; atau
- kemudahan prosedural yang mengesampingkan hak.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_scope_challenge.md bytes=2462 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Scope and Challenge

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This principle applies to every constitutional process, including:

- governance and implementation decisions;
- enforcement and standing assessment;
- forum proceedings;
- emergency measures and Adoption Transition plans;
- amendment procedures; and
- all administrative and operational activities under constitutional authority.

It is not limited to the tradeoff-stack context in which it also operates as an absolute floor under [§13.1.4 Constitutional Floors, Safety, and Anti-Degrading Process](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1314-constitutional-floors-safety-and-anti-degrading-process).

**Detection and challenge.** The character of a process is subject to the same [Contestability](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) and [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional) requirements as substantive outcomes. Affected parties may challenge the character of a process independently of whether the substantive outcome would otherwise be lawful. A correct outcome delivered through degrading process remains non-compliant.

## Draft translation (id)

**Cakupan.** Prinsip ini berlaku bagi setiap proses konstitusional, termasuk:

- keputusan tata kelola dan pelaksanaan;
- penegakan dan penilaian kedudukan untuk mengajukan perkara;
- proses forum;
- tindakan darurat dan rencana Transisi Adopsi;
- prosedur perubahan; dan
- seluruh kegiatan administratif dan operasional di bawah kewenangan konstitusional.

Prinsip ini tidak terbatas pada konteks tumpukan pertukaran kepentingan; di sana prinsip ini juga menjadi batas minimum mutlak berdasarkan [§13.1.4 Lantai konstitusional, keselamatan, dan proses tanpa degradasi](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1314-constitutional-floors-safety-and-anti-degrading-process).

**Deteksi dan gugatan.** Karakter suatu proses tunduk pada persyaratan [dapat digugat](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) dan [pengawasan](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional) yang sama dengan hasil substantif. Pihak terdampak dapat menggugat karakter proses secara terpisah dari apakah hasil substantifnya seandainya sah. Hasil yang benar tetapi disampaikan melalui proses yang merendahkan tetap tidak patuh.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__anti_degrading_what_it_is.md bytes=1698 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — What It Is

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**What it is:** a cross-cutting character floor under [§3 Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim) — it does not add substantive work of its own the way [§3.1 Fairness](#31-fairness) and [§3.2 Recognition, Reinforcement, and Aspiration](#32-recognition-reinforcement-and-aspiration) do, but constrains *how* wellbeing work, stewardship work, and every other constitutional process may be carried out — including the three pillars of [§16 Stewardship In Depth](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and the steward role defined in [§17 Consequential Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role).

## Draft translation (id)

**Apa itu:** batas lintas-bidang mengenai karakter kerja di bawah [§3 Kesejahteraan](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim). Prinsip ini tidak menambah pekerjaan substantif tersendiri seperti [§3.1 Keadilan](#31-fairness) dan [§3.2 Pengakuan, Penguatan, dan Aspirasi](#32-recognition-reinforcement-and-aspiration), melainkan membatasi *cara* kerja kesejahteraan, pengampuan, dan setiap proses konstitusional lain dilaksanakan—termasuk tiga pilar dalam [§16 Pengampuan Mendalam](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) serta peran steward yang ditetapkan dalam [§17 Pengampuan Konsekuensial](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.6 Anti-Reward plain-language rule; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — anti-reward for anti-constitutional conduct

## English source passage

*In plain terms: no one may be rewarded for committing, hiding, or retaliating over anti-constitutional conduct, whether openly or through back doors like favors, quiet promotions, or looking the other way. Helping the sentients who were harmed, and protecting those who report honestly, is different. That is the right response, and it should be encouraged.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: tidak seorang pun boleh diberi imbalan karena melakukan atau menyembunyikan tindakan yang bertentangan dengan konstitusi, atau membalas orang yang melaporkannya dengan jujur—baik secara terbuka maupun melalui jalur terselubung seperti pilih kasih, promosi diam-diam, atau pura-pura tidak melihat. Membantu sentien yang dirugikan dan melindungi pelapor yang jujur adalah hal berbeda. Itulah tanggapan yang benar dan patut didorong.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2.2 — Durable-Power Prohibition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.2 Anti-Domination Prohibitions (Don'ts)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

None of the following:
- sentient(s);
- institution(s);
- platform(s);
- enterprise(s);
- state organ(s);
- steward(s);
- coordinated group(s)

may:
- create;
- maintain;
- acquire;
- exploit;
- conceal;
- restructure around

durable power of any of the following kinds:
- market;
- platform;
- infrastructure;
- labor;
- supplier;
- data;
- compute;
- credentialing;
- capital-access;
- resource-control.

where that power foreseeably degrades:
- wellbeing;
- meaningful agency;
- fair compensation;
- innovation;
- access;
- ecological integrity;
- contestability;
- constitutional review.


## Draft translation (id)

Tidak satu pun dari pihak berikut:
- makhluk berakal;
- institusi;
- platform;
- perusahaan;
- organ negara;
- pengampu;
- kelompok terkoordinasi

boleh:
- menciptakan;
- mempertahankan;
- memperoleh;
- mengeksploitasi;
- menyembunyikan;
- menyusun ulang di sekeliling

kekuasaan yang bertahan lama dalam jenis apa pun berikut:
- pasar;
- platform;
- infrastruktur;
- tenaga kerja;
- pemasok;
- data;
- komputasi;
- kredensial;
- akses modal;
- kendali sumber daya.

jika kekuasaan itu dapat diperkirakan akan merusak:
- kesejahteraan;
- agensi yang bermakna;
- kompensasi yang adil;
- inovasi;
- akses;
- integritas ekologis;
- ketergugatan;
- peninjauan konstitusional.

# Chapter One §11.2.2 — Anti-Domination Plain-Language Rule

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.2 Anti-Domination Prohibitions (Don'ts)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms (don'ts): no locking sentients in, blocking rivals, suppressing fair bargaining, or capturing constitutional accountability.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana (hal yang tidak boleh dilakukan): jangan mengunci makhluk berakal, menghalangi pesaing, menekan perundingan yang adil, atau menguasai akuntabilitas konstitusional.*

# Chapter One §11.2.3 — Remedies for Substantiated Domination

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.3 Remedies

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when domination is substantiated, the response must fit the harm, restore real choice, and not punish size for its own sake.*

The following live in **CJS-5.125 §11.2 — Anti-domination conduct and remediation catalog**:
- illustrative prohibited conduct patterns;
- proportionate remedy tools;
- cross-domain evaluation routing.

Other owners:
- labor-mobility categorical prohibitions: [Article III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Labor and Economic Floor*);
- interoperability, portability, and exit-integrity operational terms: **CJS-5.130 §17** (*Continuity: interoperability, portability, and exit-integrity terms*);
- horizontal and vertical consolidation risk patterns: **§11.3** (*Consolidation Ceiling*).

Remedies must:
- be proportionate to:
  - concentration;
  - dependency;
  - conduct;
  - constitutional harm;
- restore contestability where domination is substantiated;
- preserve the Chapter Six Rights Floor.

Remedies may not punish scale alone.

Operative remedy selection follows **CJS-5.125 §11.2** (*Anti-domination conduct and remediation catalog*).


## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: ketika dominasi terbukti, respons harus sebanding dengan kerugian, memulihkan pilihan yang nyata, dan tidak menghukum ukuran semata.*

Hal berikut diatur dalam **CJS-5.125 §11.2 — Katalog Perilaku Dominasi dan Pemulihan**:
- pola perilaku terlarang yang bersifat ilustratif;
- perangkat pemulihan yang proporsional;
- perutean evaluasi lintas bidang.

Pemilik aturan lainnya:
- larangan kategoris mengenai mobilitas tenaga kerja: [Pasal III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Batas Minimum Hak Buruh dan Ekonomi*);
- ketentuan operasional interoperabilitas, portabilitas, dan integritas keluar: **CJS-5.130 §17** (*Kontinuitas: ketentuan interoperabilitas, portabilitas, dan integritas keluar*);
- pola risiko konsolidasi horizontal dan vertikal: **§11.3** (*Batas Konsolidasi*).

Pemulihan harus:
- sebanding dengan:
  - pemusatan;
  - ketergantungan;
  - perilaku;
  - kerugian konstitusional;
- memulihkan daya untuk menggugat ketika dominasi terbukti;
- mempertahankan Batas Minimum Hak Bab Enam.

Pemulihan tidak boleh menghukum skala semata.

Pemilihan pemulihan operasional mengikuti **CJS-5.125 §11.2**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — aspiration

## English source passage

**Aspiration** covers stated preferences and ends pursued within [§7 Freedom](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). It is part of wellbeing when consistent with dignity, equal standing, Safety, Truth, and [Substantive Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

Within those bounds, systems should **recognize and support** what sentients lawfully want to pursue.

Wanting something is not a free pass. Where consent, dignity, Safety, Truth, substantive fairness, or **Chapter Six** protections are materially at stake, those pursuits remain subject to Constitutional Collision analysis like any other constitutional claim.

## Draft translation (id)

**Aspirasi** mencakup preferensi yang dinyatakan dan tujuan yang dikejar dalam kerangka [§7 Kebebasan](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). Aspirasi menjadi bagian dari kesejahteraan apabila selaras dengan martabat, kesetaraan kedudukan, Keselamatan, Kebenaran, dan [Keadilan Substantif](core_05.055_band_participation.md#substantive-fairness).

Dalam batas tersebut, sistem sebaiknya **mengakui dan mendukung** hal-hal yang ingin dikejar para sentien secara sah.

Menginginkan sesuatu bukanlah izin tanpa batas. Jika persetujuan, martabat, Keselamatan, Kebenaran, keadilan substantif, atau perlindungan **Bab Enam** dipertaruhkan secara material, aspirasi itu tetap tunduk pada analisis Benturan Konstitusional seperti klaim konstitusional lainnya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_cluster_trigger.md bytes=1213 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Full Definition Cluster Trigger

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**When the full definition cluster applies.** The anti-segmentation rule applies when [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly), [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization), [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), or [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) is material in a way that makes those questions travel together.

## Draft translation (id)

**Kapan seluruh kelompok definisi berlaku.** Aturan anti-segmentasi berlaku ketika [Majelis](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly), [Organisasi Kolektif](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization), [Penciptaan Sistem](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), atau [Penciptaan Usaha](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) menjadi hal material yang membuat pertanyaan-pertanyaan tersebut harus dipertimbangkan bersama.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_definition_cluster_routing.md bytes=2503 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Definition Cluster Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Where the full rules live.** Chapter Five groups related definitions that must be read together when the questions they cover travel together. That grouping is a **definition cluster**. It is not a separate right, and it is not a substitute for the Chapter Six articles below. The definitions for this topic live in [Chapter Five — Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly-collective-organization-and-institutional-formation):
- [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly)
- [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization)
- [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)
- [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)

The cluster's own reading rules live there. **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) applies the [Anti-Segmentation Principle](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1511-anti-segmentation-principle) to this topic; it does not restate those Chapter Five mechanics.

## Draft translation (id)

**Di mana aturan lengkapnya.** Bab Lima mengelompokkan definisi terkait yang harus dibaca bersama ketika pertanyaan yang dicakupnya muncul secara bersamaan. Pengelompokan itu adalah **klaster definisi**. Klaster ini bukan hak yang terpisah dan bukan pengganti Pasal-Pasal Bab Enam di bawah. Definisi untuk topik ini terdapat dalam [Bab Lima — Berkumpul, Organisasi Kolektif, dan Pembentukan Institusi](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly-collective-organization-and-institutional-formation):
- [Berkumpul](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly)
- [Organisasi Kolektif](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization)
- [Penciptaan Sistem](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation)
- [Penciptaan Usaha](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation)

Aturan untuk membaca klaster itu sendiri juga terdapat di sana. **§7.2** (*Berkumpul, Organisasi Kolektif, dan Pembentukan Institusi*) menerapkan [Prinsip Anti-Segmentasi](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1511-anti-segmentation-principle) pada topik ini; bagian tersebut tidak mengulang mekanisme Bab Lima.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_lighter_rules.md bytes=1750 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Lighter Rules for Single Questions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**When lighter rules apply.** If the matter is only one of those questions — for example, an ordinary civic gathering with no labor-organizing or institution-forming stake:

- Use [Assembly](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly) or [Collective Organization](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization) as an ordinary supporting definition.
- Do not pull in [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation), or the rest of this definition cluster.
- Do not apply the anti-segmentation package of **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) just because one of those terms appears.

## Draft translation (id)

**Kapan aturan yang lebih ringan berlaku.** Jika persoalannya hanya salah satu pertanyaan itu — misalnya, pertemuan warga biasa tanpa kepentingan pengorganisasian tenaga kerja atau pembentukan lembaga — maka:
- Gunakan [Majelis](core_05.055_band_participation.md#assembly) atau [Organisasi Kolektif](core_05.055_band_participation.md#collective-organization) sebagai definisi pendukung biasa.
- Jangan memasukkan [Penciptaan Sistem](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation), [Penciptaan Usaha](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation), atau bagian lain dari kelompok definisi tersebut.
- Jangan menerapkan paket anti-segmentasi **§7.2** hanya karena salah satu istilah itu muncul.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_plain_language_nonsegmentation.md bytes=1247 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Plain-Language Assembly and Non-Segmentation

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you cannot chop assembly, union-style organizing, platform access, or permission-to-operate questions into separate boxes in a way that keeps the paperwork friendly but defeats real collective action. This section does not replace the Rights Floor: **Article XI-D** (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*) still owns assembly, and **Article III-C** (*Labor and Economic Floor*) still owns labor organizing.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: persoalan berkumpul, pengorganisasian seperti serikat pekerja, akses platform, atau izin beroperasi tidak boleh dipisah-pisahkan ke dalam kotak yang membuat dokumennya tampak tertib tetapi menggagalkan tindakan kolektif yang nyata. Bagian ini tidak menggantikan Lantai Hak: **Pasal XI-D** (*Berkumpul, Perbedaan Pendapat, dan Protes Damai*) tetap mengatur hak berkumpul, dan **Pasal III-C** (*Lantai Ketenagakerjaan dan Ekonomi*) tetap mengatur pengorganisasian tenaga kerja.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__assembly_rights_article_map.md bytes=3899 -->
# Chapter One §7.2 — Controlling Rights Article Map

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.2 Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Which rights articles still control.** **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*) is a Chapter One principle. It does not replace the Chapter Six Rights Floor. Inside **§7.2** (*Assembly, Collective Organization, and Institutional Formation*), those articles still decide what the right is and how it may be limited:
- **[Article XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-d-assembly-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*)** — gathering, associating, and acting together in physical, digital, or shared-compute spaces for expression, politics, culture, community, and similar purposes
- **[Article III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Labor and Economic Floor*)** — collective organization in productive and economic activity (unions, cooperatives, guilds, worker councils, and comparable forms used to shape the terms of work)
- **[Article XI-E](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation) (*Institutional Formation and Business Creation*)** — [System Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation) (forming and running non-commercial institutions) and [Business Creation](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) (forming and running commercial enterprises)
- **[Article X-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-x-c-stakeholder-role-and-participation-rights) (*Stakeholder Role and Participation Rights*)** and **[Article XII](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xii-stakeholder-system-participation-representation-and-due-process) (*Stakeholder System Participation, Representation, and Due Process*)** — stakeholder rights once a created institution or enterprise materially affects others

## Draft translation (id)

**Pasal hak mana yang tetap mengatur.** **§7.2** (*Berkumpul, Organisasi Kolektif, dan Pembentukan Institusi*) adalah prinsip Bab Satu. Bagian ini tidak menggantikan Lantai Hak Bab Enam. Di dalam **§7.2**, pasal-pasal berikut tetap menentukan apa haknya dan bagaimana hak itu dapat dibatasi:
- **[Pasal XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-d-assembly-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Berkumpul, Perbedaan Pendapat, dan Protes Damai*)** — berkumpul, berasosiasi, dan bertindak bersama di ruang fisik, digital, atau komputasi bersama untuk tujuan ekspresi, politik, budaya, komunitas, dan tujuan serupa.
- **[Pasal III-C](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iii-c-labor-and-economic-floor) (*Lantai Ketenagakerjaan dan Ekonomi*)** — organisasi kolektif dalam kegiatan produktif dan ekonomi (serikat pekerja, koperasi, gilda, dewan pekerja, dan bentuk sebanding untuk membentuk syarat kerja).
- **[Pasal XI-E](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-e-institutional-formation-and-business-creation) (*Pembentukan Institusi dan Penciptaan Usaha*)** — [Penciptaan Sistem](core_05.055_band_participation.md#system-creation) (membentuk dan menjalankan institusi nonkomersial) dan [Penciptaan Usaha](core_05.055_band_participation.md#business-creation) (membentuk dan menjalankan perusahaan komersial).
- **[Pasal X-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-x-c-stakeholder-role-and-participation-rights) (*Hak Peran dan Partisipasi Pemangku Kepentingan*)** dan **[Pasal XII](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xii-stakeholder-system-participation-representation-and-due-process) (*Partisipasi, Representasi, dan Proses Hukum yang Patut dalam Sistem bagi Pemangku Kepentingan*)** — hak pemangku kepentingan ketika institusi atau usaha yang dibentuk berdampak material terhadap orang lain.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — benefits and burdens

## English source passage

This subsection sets out when the sharing of benefits and burdens is fair:

- A system is not fair when some sentients receive the gains while others quietly absorb the costs.
- Favoritism, hidden cost-shifting, selective enforcement, and scoreboard tricks do not satisfy this requirement.

## Draft translation (id)

Subbagian ini menjelaskan kapan pembagian manfaat dan beban bersifat adil:

- Sistem tidak adil jika sebagian sentien menerima keuntungan sementara yang lain diam-diam menanggung biayanya.
- Favoritisme, pengalihan biaya tersembunyi, penegakan selektif, dan rekayasa papan skor tidak memenuhi persyaratan ini.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — links to fairness and capability rules

## English source

This builds on [§3.1.1 Access and Opportunity](#311-access-and-opportunity) and [§3.1.3 Fair Treatment](#313-fair-treatment), and it applies [§16 Pillar 2 — Proactive stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship) to the sentients a system affects. For developing sentients it works with [Graduated Capability](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability), which judges growth by what a sentient can actually do.

## id draft

Ketentuan ini bertumpu pada [§3.1.1 Akses dan Kesempatan](#311-access-and-opportunity) serta [§3.1.3 Perlakuan Adil](#313-fair-treatment), dan menerapkan [§16 Pilar 2 — pengelolaan proaktif](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship) kepada makhluk sentien yang terdampak oleh sistem. Untuk makhluk sentien yang sedang berkembang, ketentuan ini bekerja bersama [Kapasitas Bertahap](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability), yang menilai perkembangan berdasarkan apa yang sungguh dapat dilakukan oleh makhluk sentien.

# Chapter One excerpt — consolidation ceiling definition

## English source

**Consolidation ceilings** are adopter-tunable early-warning triggers for heightened review, intervention, or structural remedy when consolidation reaches a level at which the impairments in **§11.3.1** (*Consolidation Risk, Pre-Lock-In Impairment*) are predictably imminent. They sit under the **§11** (*Market Structure*) non-concentration discipline and **§11.2** (*Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination*) anti-domination rules; they are not a ban on scale.

## id draft

**Batas konsolidasi** adalah pemicu peringatan dini yang dapat disesuaikan pengadopsi untuk meningkatkan peninjauan, melakukan intervensi, atau menerapkan pemulihan struktural ketika konsolidasi mencapai tingkat yang membuat dampak buruk dalam **§11.3.1** (*Risiko Konsolidasi, Gangguan Sebelum Penguncian*) diperkirakan segera terjadi. Batas ini berada di bawah disiplin non-konsentrasi **§11** (*Struktur Pasar*) dan aturan anti-dominasi **§11.2** (*Dukungan terhadap Persaingan dan Pencegahan Dominasi*); batas ini bukan larangan untuk menjadi besar.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — operative ceiling-setting discipline scope

## English source

Operative ceiling-setting discipline — horizontal and vertical trigger design, crossing presumption, rebuttal, anti-nullification, and remedy routing — lives in **CJS-5.125 §11.3 — Consolidation ceiling-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)**.

## id draft

Disiplin operasional penetapan batas—perancangan pemicu horizontal dan vertikal, praduga bahwa batas telah terlampaui, sanggahan, pencegahan peniadaan, dan penyaluran pemulihan—terdapat dalam **CJS-5.125 §11.3 — Disiplin Penetapan Batas Konsolidasi (dapat disesuaikan oleh pengadopsi)**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — domains requiring consolidation ceilings

## English source

Adopters must define consolidation ceilings for:
- markets;
- platforms;
- infrastructure layers;
- labor-demand markets;
- supplier or resource-control systems;
- data or compute dependencies;
- credentialing role pathways;
- capital-access channels;
- comparable domains.

## id draft

Pengadopsi harus menetapkan batas konsolidasi untuk:
- pasar;
- platform;
- lapisan infrastruktur;
- pasar permintaan tenaga kerja;
- sistem pemasok atau kendali sumber daya;
- ketergantungan data atau komputasi;
- jalur peran yang memerlukan kredensial;
- saluran akses modal;
- ranah sebanding.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__celebration_of_success.md bytes=1870 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.2 Celebration of Success; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — celebration of success

## English source passage

**Celebration** honors **traceable**, **non-misleading** accomplishment and prosocial coordination. That includes uplift and stewardship narratives tied to verified [contribution](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature).

Celebration must not:
- substitute for real progress toward underlying constitutional objectives where proxy optimization has diverged from them ([§3 Foundational Objective: Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim))
- become **capture** of acclaim or prestige ([§19 Incentive Alignment and System Capture](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture))
- excuse avoidance of accountability where Safety, Truth, or rights protections are implicated

## Draft translation (id)

**Perayaan** menghormati pencapaian yang **dapat ditelusuri**, **tidak menyesatkan**, dan koordinasi prososial. Ini mencakup narasi penyemangat dan penatalayanan yang terkait dengan [kontribusi](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contribution-nature) terverifikasi.

Perayaan tidak boleh:
- menggantikan kemajuan nyata menuju tujuan konstitusional yang mendasar ketika pengoptimalan proksi telah menyimpang darinya ([§3 Tujuan Dasar: Kesejahteraan](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim));
- menjadi **penguasaan** atas pujian atau prestise ([§19 Penyelarasan Insentif dan Penangkapan Sistem](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture));
- menjadi alasan untuk menghindari akuntabilitas ketika Keselamatan, Kebenaran, atau perlindungan hak dipertaruhkan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__certification_and_sentient_rights_safeguards.md bytes=1607 -->
# §6 Trust — Complementary Certification and Rights Safeguards

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Certification is one of two safeguards that work together: it makes a system worthy of trust, and the rights of sentients to [challenge it](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-a-reliability-and-trustworthiness-baseline) and [seek redress](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) and to [have it audited](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification) keep it honest. Neither replaces the other — [Article XIII](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Right to Reliable and Trustworthy Systems*) states how the two combine.

## Draft translation (id)

Sertifikasi adalah salah satu dari dua perlindungan yang bekerja bersama: sertifikasi membuat sistem layak dipercaya, sedangkan hak sentien untuk [menggugatnya](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-a-reliability-and-trustworthiness-baseline), [meminta pemulihan](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy), dan [mengauditnya](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification) menjaga kejujurannya. Keduanya tidak saling menggantikan—[Pasal XIII](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Hak atas Sistem yang Andal dan Layak Dipercaya*) menjelaskan bagaimana keduanya berpadu.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §5.2.4 Plain-Language Accessibility — Complexity-as-Defeat Discipline

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.4

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Systems may not use complicated language, confusing procedures, or deliberate obscurity to stop sentients from [contesting](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) decisions, using their [meaningful agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), getting to [audits](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification), or exercising their [Chapter Six](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights) rights.

The same holds for structure, not only language. Complexity may not be used — through technical, organizational, contractual, or procedural layering — to defeat audit, contest, or correction. Components, handoffs between organizations, agreements, or procedural steps may not be stacked so that no one can see through them, or so that responsibility, observability, or the path to a remedy is lost between the layers.

The opposite is also forbidden. Plain language must not misstate what a rule really does, hide its real effect, or stand in for the operative text. That breaks the [Truth](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) constraint.

## Draft translation (id)

Sistem tidak boleh menggunakan bahasa rumit, prosedur membingungkan, atau ketidakjelasan yang disengaja untuk menghalangi sentien [menggugat](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#contestability) keputusan, menggunakan [agensi bermakna](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency), mengakses [audit](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xvi-audit-transparency-and-independent-verification), atau menjalankan hak [Bab Enam](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights).

Hal yang sama berlaku untuk struktur, bukan hanya bahasa. Kompleksitas tidak boleh digunakan—melalui pelapisan teknis, organisasi, kontrak, atau prosedur—untuk menggagalkan audit, gugatan, atau koreksi. Komponen, serah terima antarorganisasi, perjanjian, atau langkah prosedural tidak boleh ditumpuk sedemikian rupa sehingga tak seorang pun dapat memahaminya, atau tanggung jawab, keteramatan, maupun jalur pemulihan hilang di antara lapisan.

Kebalikannya juga dilarang. Bahasa sederhana tidak boleh menyatakan secara keliru apa yang sebenarnya dilakukan suatu aturan, menyembunyikan dampak nyatanya, atau menggantikan teks operatif. Hal itu melanggar batasan [Kebenaran](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__concentration_misconduct_referral_threshold.md bytes=1552 -->

# Chapter One excerpt — concentration-breach referral threshold

## English source passage

- **Creating, maintaining, or exploiting concentration that breaches the requirements:** this can go to Chapter Twelve for misconduct designation review, but only in two steps. First, [Chapter Ten §4 Question 2 — how good or bad was it?](core_11.000_standing_assessment.md#4-question-2--how-good-or-bad-was-it) must already have scored the verified harm among the three most serious levels. Then Chapter Twelve checks whether the concentration meets criterion 3 (a workaround that rolls back protections), criterion 4 (damage to Rights Floor protections or blocked challenges), or criterion 6 (constitutional process made unusable in practice).

## Draft translation (id)

- **Menciptakan, mempertahankan, atau mengeksploitasi konsentrasi yang melanggar persyaratan:** rujukan untuk peninjauan pelanggaran hanya melalui dua langkah. Pertama, [Bab Sepuluh §4 Pertanyaan 2 — seberapa baik atau buruk hal itu?](core_11.000_standing_assessment.md#4-question-2--how-good-or-bad-was-it) harus menempatkan kerugian terverifikasi pada tiga tingkat paling serius; lalu Bab Dua Belas memeriksa kriteria 3 (pengakalan yang membatalkan perlindungan), 4 (merusak Lantai Hak atau menghalangi gugatan), atau 6 (membuat proses konstitusional tak dapat digunakan).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__concentration_threshold_definition.md bytes=983 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.1, concentration thresholds; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — concentration thresholds

## English source

**Concentration thresholds** are quantitative triggers that mark when concentration has reached a level requiring heightened review, intervention, or structural remedy. They cover material, jurisdictional, capability, platform, and information-sphere concentration. Adopters may tune these triggers **within the constitutional requirements**.

## id draft

**Ambang konsentrasi** adalah pemicu kuantitatif yang menandai bahwa konsentrasi telah mencapai tingkat yang memerlukan peninjauan lebih ketat, intervensi, atau pemulihan struktural. Ambang ini mencakup konsentrasi material, yurisdiksi, kemampuan, platform, dan ruang informasi. Pihak pengadopsi dapat menyesuaikan pemicu tersebut **dalam batas persyaratan konstitusional**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__connecting_not_treating.md bytes=1073 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, connecting is not treating; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — connecting to help

## English source

**Connecting is not treating:** The system's duty is to try to reach the sentient and hand them to help, such as a contact they chose, a support or care service, or the route under **Article III-B** (*Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access*). Anything done without the sentient's consent stays under **Article VII-C** (*Health Crisis and Involuntary-Intervention Floor*).

## id draft

**Menghubungi bukanlah merawat:** Kewajiban sistem adalah berupaya menjangkau makhluk sentien dan menghubungkannya dengan bantuan, seperti kontak pilihan mereka, layanan dukungan atau perawatan, atau jalur berdasarkan **Pasal III-B** (*Pemeliharaan Tubuh dan Akses Layanan Kesehatan*). Apa pun yang dilakukan tanpa persetujuan makhluk itu tetap tunduk pada **Pasal VII-C** (*Landasan Krisis Kesehatan dan Intervensi Tanpa Persetujuan*).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation ceiling mechanism

## English source passage

*In plain terms: adopters must set evidence-based ceilings that trigger scrutiny before consolidation risks in **§11.3.1** (*Consolidation Risk, Pre-Lock-In Impairment*) materialize into lock-in — with separate horizontal and vertical triggers where the domain requires it.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: pihak pengadopsi harus menetapkan batas berbasis bukti yang memicu pemeriksaan sebelum risiko konsolidasi dalam **§11.3.1** (Risiko Konsolidasi, Gangguan Sebelum Penguncian) berubah menjadi penguncian—dengan pemicu horizontal dan vertikal yang terpisah bila bidangnya memerlukan.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__consolidation_ceiling_plain_language.md bytes=860 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11.3 Consolidation Ceiling plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation ceiling

## English source passage

*In plain terms: consolidation can steal real choice long before a market looks locked down. **Consolidation ceilings** give governance and stewards an early-warning power — to scrutinize, intervene, and correct pile-ups while exit and rivalry still exist.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: konsolidasi dapat merampas pilihan nyata jauh sebelum pasar tampak terkunci. **Batas konsolidasi** memberi tata kelola dan steward kewenangan peringatan dini untuk meninjau, mengintervensi, dan memperbaiki penumpukan selagi jalan keluar dan persaingan masih ada.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__consolidation_risk_plain_language.md bytes=949 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11.3.1 Consolidation Risk plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — consolidation risk

## English source passage

*In plain terms: consolidation can hollow out real alternatives long before a market looks "locked down." Two kinds of pile-up matter especially: **horizontal consolidation** — fewer rivals at the same layer — and **vertical consolidation** — control across layers that creates chokepoints and lock-in.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: konsolidasi dapat mengosongkan alternatif nyata jauh sebelum pasar tampak “terkunci”. Dua jenis penumpukan sangat penting: **konsolidasi horizontal**—lebih sedikit pesaing pada tingkat yang sama—dan **konsolidasi vertikal**—kendali lintas tingkat yang menciptakan titik kemacetan dan penguncian.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.2 — Claim Criteria and Limits on Efficiency Gains

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**When an efficiency claim counts:**

- **Traces to:** real constitutional outcomes.
- **Stays consistent with:**
  - Safety;
  - Truth;
  - the Chapter Six Rights Floor;
  - ecological integrity;
  - dignity;
  - meaningful agency; and
  - fair distribution.

**Efficiency gains must not:**

- hollow the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad); or
- substitute dashboard metrics for progress toward the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims).


## Draft translation (id)

**Kapan klaim efisiensi dapat dihitung:**

- **Harus dapat ditelusuri ke:** hasil konstitusional yang nyata.
- **Harus tetap selaras dengan:**
  - Keselamatan;
  - Kebenaran;
  - Batas Minimum Hak Bab Enam;
  - integritas ekologis;
  - martabat;
  - agensi yang bermakna; dan
  - distribusi yang adil.

**Peningkatan efisiensi tidak boleh:**

- mengosongkan makna [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad); atau
- mengganti kemajuan menuju [Dua Tujuan Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) dengan metrik dasbor.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_definition.md bytes=1081 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Constitutional Efficiency Definition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**[Constitutional Efficiency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency)** is the other main aspect of **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. It asks whether systems produce more constitutionally required benefit per unit of sentient time, attention, effort, coordination, materials, infrastructure, and energy consumed.

## Draft translation (id)

**[Efisiensi Konstitusional](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency)** adalah aspek utama lainnya dari **[Kapasitas Sistem Bersama](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. Aspek ini menilai apakah sistem menghasilkan lebih banyak manfaat yang diwajibkan Konstitusi untuk setiap unit waktu, perhatian, upaya, koordinasi, bahan, infrastruktur, dan energi yang dikonsumsi makhluk berakal.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_not.md bytes=864 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Efficiency Is Not a Proxy Goal

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Efficiency can drive broadly shared improvement, but only inside constitutional bounds. By itself, it is **not**:
- raw speed;
- administrative convenience;
- utilization targets;
- revenue growth;
- market share;
- headcount; or
- cost-cutting for its own sake.

## Draft translation (id)

Efisiensi dapat mendorong perbaikan yang manfaatnya dibagi luas, tetapi hanya dalam batas-batas konstitusional. Efisiensi saja **bukanlah**:
- kecepatan semata;
- kemudahan administratif;
- target pemanfaatan;
- pertumbuhan pendapatan;
- pangsa pasar;
- jumlah tenaga kerja; atau
- pemangkasan biaya demi pemangkasan itu sendiri.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__constitutional_efficiency_plain_language.md bytes=985 -->
# Chapter One §9.2 — Constitutional Efficiency Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.2 Constitutional Efficiency

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: constitutional efficiency is the "are we getting our money's worth in human terms?" aspect of shared-system capacity. More real benefit for each hour of sentient time, attention, and shared effort — not cutting corners on rights, truth, safety, or ecology just to look fast, lean, or cheap.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Efisiensi Konstitusional adalah sisi kapasitas sistem bersama yang bertanya, “Apakah upaya yang kita keluarkan benar-benar memberi manfaat bagi kehidupan?” Lebih banyak manfaat nyata untuk setiap jam waktu, perhatian, dan upaya bersama makhluk berakal—tanpa mengorbankan hak, kebenaran, keselamatan, atau ekologi hanya agar tampak cepat, ramping, atau murah.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — contact data limits

## English source

**Contacts and data:** A trusted contact is used only if the sentient chose it in advance, and only the information needed to get a response is shared. Outreach uses contact details the system already holds for that purpose and is not a reason to collect more data.

## id draft

**Kontak dan data:** Kontak tepercaya hanya digunakan jika makhluk sentien telah memilihnya sebelumnya, dan hanya informasi yang diperlukan untuk memperoleh tanggapan yang dibagikan. Upaya menghubungi menggunakan detail kontak yang sudah dimiliki sistem untuk tujuan itu dan bukan alasan untuk mengumpulkan lebih banyak data.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__contestability_in_practice.md bytes=1089 -->
# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Contestability in Practice

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Shared-system capacity must stay contestable in practice. Under the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), domination that hollows **participation**, **oversight**, **accountability**, or **timeliness** — scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) — is incompatible with this section, whatever the scale or efficiency claim.

## Draft translation (id)

Kapasitas sistem bersama harus tetap dapat digugat dalam praktik. Berdasarkan [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), dominasi yang mengosongkan **partisipasi**, **pengawasan**, **akuntabilitas**, atau **ketepatan waktu**—dengan skala yang sebanding dengan [kepentingan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)—tidak sesuai dengan bagian ini, apa pun skala atau klaim efisiensinya.

# Chapter One §8 — Continuity in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §8 Continuity Aim

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Continuity is the second of the two aims: making sure the good outcome lasts — across failures, across generations, and without a few powerful actors locking it up. It opens with Shared-System Capacity, which serves Flourishing and is also the substance of Continuity.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Kontinuitas adalah tujuan kedua dari dua tujuan, yaitu memastikan hasil yang baik bertahan melintasi kegagalan dan generasi tanpa dikuasai segelintir pihak yang kuat. Tujuan ini dimulai dengan Kapasitas Sistem Bersama, yang mendukung Kemajuan dan sekaligus merupakan substansi Kontinuitas.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__continuity_principle_hierarchy.md bytes=3825 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A principle hierarchy for Continuity; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Continuity principle hierarchy

## English source

**Principle hierarchy (Continuity).** At principle layer, the Continuity aim is developed in this order, opening with the capacity that bridges the two aims:

6. **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)** is what good stewardship, governance, and incentives should add up to over time — real, challengeable ability for sentients and shared systems to get constitutionally required work done. It straddles the two aims: it is a means toward **Flourishing** and the substance of **Continuity**, and it is not a trump card over everything else. **[§9.1](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)** and **[§9.2](#92-constitutional-efficiency)** explain its two main aspects.
7. **[Resilience and Self-Healing Design](#10-resilience-and-self-healing-design)** states how systems that sentients depend on detect trouble early, contain it, fail along disclosed paths, and recover honestly (**[Self-Healing](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)**), so the capacity from item 6 stays durable and stability does not depend on emergency intervention.
8. **[Market Structure](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-structure)** at [§11](#11-market-structure) supplies the anti-concentration discipline that keeps that capacity contestable in practice.
9. **[Systemic Evaluation Requirement](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement)** verifies whole-system scope, dependency, and incentive alignment before compliance or governance claims stand — under the **oversight** Tetrad leg as principle-layer orientation for auditing, including [System Alignment Certification](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) as one especially large audit process among others.

## id draft

**Hierarki prinsip (Continuity).** Pada tingkat prinsip, arah Continuity dikembangkan dengan urutan berikut, dimulai dari kapasitas yang menjembatani kedua arah:

6. **[Kapasitas Sistem Bersama](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)** adalah hasil yang seharusnya terbentuk dari penatalayanan, tata kelola, dan insentif yang baik dari waktu ke waktu: kemampuan nyata dan dapat digugat bagi para sentien serta sistem bersama untuk melaksanakan pekerjaan yang diwajibkan konstitusi. Kapasitas ini menjembatani kedua arah: sarana menuju Flourishing dan substansi Continuity, tetapi bukan kartu truf atas segalanya. Dua aspeknya dijelaskan dalam [§9.1](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good) dan [§9.2](#92-constitutional-efficiency).
7. **[Desain Ketahanan dan Pemulihan Mandiri](#10-resilience-and-self-healing-design)** menjelaskan bagaimana sistem yang diandalkan sentien mendeteksi masalah lebih awal, membatasinya, gagal melalui jalur yang diungkapkan, dan pulih secara jujur (**[Pemulihan Mandiri](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)**), agar kapasitas butir 6 bertahan dan stabilitas tidak bergantung pada intervensi darurat.
8. **[Struktur Pasar](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-structure)** pada [§11](#11-market-structure) menyediakan disiplin antikonsentrasi agar kapasitas itu tetap dapat digugat dalam praktik.
9. **[Persyaratan Evaluasi Sistemik](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement)** memeriksa ruang lingkup seluruh sistem, ketergantungan, dan keselarasan insentif sebelum klaim kepatuhan atau tata kelola diterima—sebagai orientasi prinsip untuk audit di bawah kaki **pengawasan** Tetrad, termasuk [Sertifikasi Keselarasan Sistem](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#system-alignment-certification) sebagai salah satu proses audit besar di antara proses lainnya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__correction_and_remedy_duty.md bytes=2089 -->
# §6.1 Trust — Correction and Remedy Duty

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when a system does wrong, it fixes it. A system is not trustworthy because it never fails; it is trustworthy because failures are acknowledged, corrected, and repaired — in time, by capacity that actually exists.*

No system that sentients depend on will be free of failure. What makes reliance on it warranted is what happens next. Correction is part of [**Trust**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust), not an extra: a system that does wrong and does not fix it cannot keep the trust it asks for.

Where a system's failure materially harms sentients or fails a Chapter Six Rights Floor, those responsible for the system must:

- acknowledge the failure;
- correct it;
- repair the harm proportionately (see [Redress and Remediation](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation)); and
- act to prevent it happening again.

## Draft translation (id)

*Sederhananya: ketika sistem melakukan kesalahan, sistem memperbaikinya. Sistem dapat dipercaya bukan karena tidak pernah gagal, melainkan karena kegagalannya diakui, dikoreksi, dan dipulihkan—tepat waktu, dengan kapasitas yang benar-benar tersedia.*

Tidak ada sistem yang diandalkan sentien yang bebas dari kegagalan. Yang membuat ketergantungan padanya beralasan adalah apa yang terjadi setelahnya. Koreksi merupakan bagian dari [**Kepercayaan**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust), bukan tambahan: sistem yang berbuat salah dan tidak memperbaikinya tidak dapat mempertahankan kepercayaan yang dimintanya.

Jika kegagalan sistem menimbulkan kerugian material bagi sentien atau melanggar Lantai Hak Bab Enam, pihak yang bertanggung jawab atas sistem wajib:

- mengakui kegagalan;
- memperbaikinya;
- memulihkan kerugian secara proporsional (lihat [Pemulihan dan Remediasi](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#redress-and-remediation)); dan
- mengambil tindakan agar hal itu tidak terulang.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__correction_real_capacity_timing_cost.md bytes=1784 -->
# §6.1 Trust — Real Remedy Capacity, Timing, and Cost

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

That correction must be real:
- **Real capacity:** Remedy is delivered by a [Remedy System](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#remedy-system) — durable, staffed, funded, and accessible capacity — not a remedy that exists only on paper.
- **In time:** Remedy that arrives only after harm has become irreversible, or after the sentient has been worn down into giving up, is not remedy (see [Timely Resolution](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution)).
- **Borne by those responsible:** The cost of correction is not shifted onto those harmed, their communities, or public remedy systems where the responsible actors can bear it. Expense, restructuring, or succession does not by itself end the duty.

## Draft translation (id)

Koreksi itu harus nyata:
- **Kapasitas nyata:** Pemulihan diberikan melalui [Sistem Pemulihan](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#remedy-system)—kapasitas yang berkelanjutan, memiliki staf dan pendanaan, serta dapat diakses—bukan pemulihan yang hanya ada di atas kertas.
- **Tepat waktu:** Pemulihan yang baru datang setelah kerugian menjadi tidak dapat dipulihkan, atau setelah sentien dibuat letih hingga menyerah, bukanlah pemulihan (lihat [Penyelesaian Tepat Waktu](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#timely-resolution)).
- **Ditanggung pihak yang bertanggung jawab:** Biaya koreksi tidak dialihkan kepada pihak yang dirugikan, komunitas mereka, atau sistem pemulihan publik jika pihak yang bertanggung jawab mampu menanggungnya. Biaya, restrukturisasi, atau suksesi tidak dengan sendirinya mengakhiri kewajiban.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__correction_rights_implementation_refs.md bytes=1377 -->
# §6.1 Trust — Right to Correction and Implementation References

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The right to this correction is stated in [Article XIII-B](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) (*Right to Redress and Remedy*) in Chapter Six. [Chapter Eleven §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (*Enforcement realism and remedy systems*) applies this principle to standing consequences, and [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*) sets institutional implementation.

## Draft translation (id)

Hak atas koreksi ini dinyatakan dalam [Pasal XIII-B](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy) (*Hak atas Ganti Rugi dan Pemulihan*) di Bab Enam. [Bab Sebelas §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (*Realisme Penegakan dan Sistem Pemulihan*) menerapkan prinsip ini pada konsekuensi kedudukan hukum, dan [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Sistem Pemulihan dan Kapasitas Pemulihan Kelembagaan*) menetapkan pelaksanaan kelembagaan.

# §6.1 Trust — Trace and Reading Relationships

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6.1 Trace

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- Read with: [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad) — **accountability** leg (answerability, redress, and real correction); **timeliness** leg (remedy before delay makes it unreachable); [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) scaling for remedy depth.
- Read with: [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) — **Flourishing** aim (reliance stays warranted because failures are put right); **Continuity** aim (durable remedy capacity across time, succession, and restructuring).
- Upstream: Principles: [4 Safety](#4-safety-harm-constraint), [5 Truth](#5-truth-epistemic-integrity-constraint), and [§6 Trust](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity).
- Downstream: [Article XIII-B: Right to Redress and Remedy](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy); [Chapter Eleven §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (11.000 §9) (*Enforcement realism and remedy systems*) for standing consequences; [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Remedy systems and institutional redress capacity*) for institutional implementation.

## Draft translation (id)

- Baca bersama [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad): unsur **akuntabilitas** (pertanggungjawaban, ganti rugi, dan koreksi nyata); unsur **ketepatan waktu** (pemulihan sebelum penundaan membuatnya tidak terjangkau); [taruhan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) menentukan kedalaman pemulihan.
- Baca bersama [Dua Tujuan Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims): **Flourishing** (ketergantungan tetap beralasan karena kegagalan diperbaiki); **Continuity** (kapasitas pemulihan yang bertahan sepanjang waktu, suksesi, dan restrukturisasi).
- Prinsip hulu: [4 Keselamatan](#4-safety-harm-constraint), [5 Kebenaran](#5-truth-epistemic-integrity-constraint), dan [§6 Kepercayaan](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity).
- Penerapan hilir: [Pasal XIII-B: Hak atas Ganti Rugi dan Pemulihan](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-b-right-to-redress-and-remedy); [Bab Sebelas §9](core_12.000_standing_integration.md#9-enforcement-realism-and-remedy-systems) (*Realisme Penegakan dan Sistem Pemulihan*) untuk konsekuensi kedudukan hukum; serta [CI-11.205](corpus_institutions/ci_12.205_remedy_systems_institutional_redress_capacity.md) (*Sistem Pemulihan dan Kapasitas Pemulihan Kelembagaan*) untuk pelaksanaan kelembagaan.

# §5.2.3 Plain-Language Accessibility — Definitional Rigor Preserved

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Plain-language work is **not** a license to soften definitional rigor. These remain controlling at the definition layer:

- Chapter Five definitions and their O/M/A/C components;
- Chapter Two through Four definition mechanics.

Writing something in simpler language does not change what it means. If a plain-language summary and the formal definition it summarizes appear to say different things, the formal definition controls — and the summary must be corrected to match.

## Draft translation (id)

Penulisan dengan bahasa sederhana **bukan** izin untuk melonggarkan ketelitian definisi. Hal-hal berikut tetap mengendalikan pada lapisan definisi:

- definisi Bab Lima beserta komponen O/M/A/C-nya;
- mekanisme definisi Bab Dua sampai Empat.

Menuliskan sesuatu dengan bahasa yang lebih sederhana tidak mengubah maknanya. Jika ringkasan bahasa sederhana dan definisi formal yang diringkas tampak menyatakan hal berbeda, definisi formal yang berlaku—dan ringkasannya harus diperbaiki agar sesuai.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
