# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent Keeps Systems Contestable

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent keeps systems contestable.** The [**Continuity**](core_00.000_preamble.md#continuity) aim depends on constitutional systems that stay contestable. Dissent and peaceful protest are how contest reaches authority from outside formal process, and how errors that formal process has missed become visible. They serve the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad) **participation** and **oversight** legs.

## Draft translation (id)

**Perbedaan pendapat menjaga agar sistem tetap dapat digugat.** Tujuan [Kontinuitas](core_00.000_preamble.md#continuity) bergantung pada sistem konstitusional yang tetap dapat digugat. Perbedaan pendapat dan protes damai menyampaikan gugatan kepada pihak berwenang dari luar proses formal serta menampakkan kesalahan yang luput dari proses tersebut. Keduanya melayani pilar **partisipasi** dan **pengawasan** dalam [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_limit_discipline.md bytes=1209 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Limiting Dissent

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Limiting dissent follows limitation discipline.** Dissent and peaceful protest may be limited only under [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).
- Disagreement, disruption, inconvenience, offense, unpopularity, or pressure on authority is not, by itself, **material harm** or **systemic risk**.
- Limits must not turn on viewpoint, and the party limiting dissent bears the burden under **Chapter Four**.

## Draft translation (id)

**Pembatasan perbedaan pendapat mengikuti disiplin pembatasan.** Perbedaan pendapat dan protes damai hanya boleh dibatasi berdasarkan [§7.1 Disiplin Pembatasan](#71-limitation-discipline).
- Ketidaksetujuan, gangguan, ketidaknyamanan, penghinaan, ketidakpopuleran, atau tekanan terhadap otoritas tidak dengan sendirinya merupakan **bahaya material** atau **risiko sistemik**.
- Pembatasan tidak boleh didasarkan pada sudut pandang, dan pihak yang membatasi perbedaan pendapat memikul beban pembuktian menurut **Bab Empat**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_meaningful_agency.md bytes=1000 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent as Meaningful Agency

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent is part of freedom.** [Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) includes the ability to disagree with, object to, and organize against the decisions, institutions, and systems that shape a sentient's life. Bounded agency that could not reach the systems doing the bounding would not be meaningful.

## Draft translation (id)

**Perbedaan pendapat adalah bagian dari kebebasan.** [Agensi Bermakna](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency) mencakup kemampuan untuk tidak setuju, mengajukan keberatan, dan berorganisasi melawan keputusan, lembaga, dan sistem yang membentuk kehidupan makhluk berkesadaran. Agensi terbatas yang tidak dapat menjangkau sistem yang membatasinya bukanlah agensi yang bermakna.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_not_subversion.md bytes=1420 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent Is Not Subversion

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Dissent is not subversion.** Disagreeing with this Constitution or with the authority acting under it, and advocating by peaceful and lawful means to amend it or to change who holds authority, is exercise of freedom. It is not the [subversion](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#subversion) of constitutional systems excluded from protected agency in [§7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). Subversion requires force, coercion, usurpation, or making constitutional process or remedies unusable in practice.

## Draft translation (id)

**Perbedaan pendapat bukan subversi.** Tidak setuju dengan Konstitusi ini atau dengan otoritas yang bertindak di bawahnya, serta mengadvokasi perubahan Konstitusi atau pergantian pemegang otoritas melalui cara damai dan sah, merupakan pelaksanaan kebebasan. Hal itu bukan [subversi](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#subversion) terhadap sistem konstitusional yang dikecualikan dari agensi terlindungi dalam [§7 Kebebasan (Agensi Terbatas)](#7-freedom-bounded-agency). Subversi mensyaratkan kekerasan, pemaksaan, perebutan kekuasaan, atau membuat proses atau pemulihan konstitusional tidak dapat digunakan dalam praktik.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_plain_terms.md bytes=874 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Dissent in Plain Terms

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom includes the freedom to say no — to disagree with any authority, including this Constitution, and to protest peacefully to change it. A system that punishes disagreement is no longer contestable, and a system that cannot be contested cannot correct itself.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: kebebasan mencakup kebebasan untuk berkata tidak — tidak setuju dengan otoritas mana pun, termasuk Konstitusi ini, dan memprotes secara damai untuk mengubahnya. Sistem yang menghukum perbedaan pendapat tidak lagi dapat digugat; sistem yang tidak dapat digugat tidak dapat memperbaiki dirinya sendiri.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_scope.md bytes=558 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Scope Limit

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This subsection does not protect separable conduct that independently breaches Safety or another sentient's Rights-Floor minimums.

## Draft translation (id)

**Cakupan.** Subbagian ini tidak melindungi tindakan terpisah yang secara mandiri melanggar Keselamatan atau batas minimum Lantai Hak makhluk berkesadaran lain.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_standing_voice.md bytes=1363 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Freedom Must Not Cost Standing or Voice

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Exercising freedom must not cost standing or voice.** A sentient's exercise of this freedom must not become a reason to lower its standing, narrow its role eligibility, reduce its governance voice, or count against a system's alignment certification. Operative rules, including the civil-disobedience rule and the burden on adverse actions that follow dissent, live in **[Article XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#xi-d-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Assembly, Dissent, and Peaceful Protest*)**.

## Draft translation (id)

**Menjalankan kebebasan tidak boleh mengorbankan kedudukan atau suara.** Pelaksanaan kebebasan ini oleh makhluk berkesadaran tidak boleh dijadikan alasan untuk menurunkan kedudukannya, mempersempit kelayakan perannya, mengurangi suara tata kelolanya, atau diperhitungkan untuk merugikan sertifikasi keselarasan sistem. Aturan operatif, termasuk aturan pembangkangan sipil dan beban atas tindakan merugikan setelah perbedaan pendapat, terdapat dalam **[Pasal XI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#xi-d-dissent-and-peaceful-protest) (*Majelis, Perbedaan Pendapat, dan Protes Damai*)**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__dissent_system_evaluation.md bytes=1104 -->
# Chapter One §7.3 — Whole-System Dissent Check

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

When a whole system is being evaluated, the evaluation must check how it treats dissent and peaceful protest, using [Chapter Nine §4.3.1 Dissent and Peaceful Protest](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#431-dissent-and-peaceful-protest). Until that check is done, no one can claim the system is properly classified, well governed, or compliant where this section applies.

## Draft translation (id)

Saat seluruh sistem dievaluasi, evaluasi harus memeriksa perlakuannya terhadap perbedaan pendapat dan protes damai, dengan merujuk [Bab Sembilan §4.3.1 Perbedaan Pendapat dan Protes Damai](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#431-dissent-and-peaceful-protest). Sampai pemeriksaan itu selesai, tidak boleh ada klaim bahwa sistem diklasifikasikan dengan benar, dikelola dengan baik, atau patuh ketika bagian ini berlaku.

# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Limits and Evidence

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Limiting expression is different from using it as evidence.** A limit on expression must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) and **[Article XI-B](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-b-expression) (*Expression*)**, and a limit aimed at what is said faces the strictest review there. Using what a sentient has said as evidence of intent, purpose, or authority is not a limit. It follows the evidence rules of the chapter that asks the question, such as [Chapter Seven §5 Formation Check: Office-Holder Standing and Authenticity](core_07.000_charters.md#5-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity), and it may not become a penalty for the speech itself.

## Draft translation (id)

**Membatasi ekspresi berbeda dari menggunakannya sebagai bukti.** Pembatasan ekspresi harus memenuhi [§7.1 Disiplin Pembatasan](#71-limitation-discipline) dan **[Pasal XI-B](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-b-expression) (*Ekspresi*)**; pembatasan yang menargetkan apa yang dikatakan tunduk pada peninjauan paling ketat di sana. Menggunakan ucapan makhluk berkesadaran sebagai bukti niat, tujuan, atau kewenangan bukanlah pembatasan. Penggunaan itu mengikuti aturan pembuktian bab yang mengajukan pertanyaan, seperti [Bab Tujuh §5 Pemeriksaan Pembentukan: Kedudukan dan Keaslian Pemegang Jabatan](core_07.000_charters.md#5-formation-check-office-holder-standing-and-authenticity), dan tidak boleh menjadi hukuman atas ucapan itu sendiri.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_labels_topics.md bytes=1638 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Labels and Topics Do Not Change the Test

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Labels and topics do not change the test.** A religious, political, ideological, cultural, artistic, or commercial label does not turn expression that harms others, or treats them in ways forbidden under **[Article VI-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-c-nondiscrimination) (*Nondiscrimination*)**, into protected expression. A topic or point of view does not turn protected expression into harm. **[Article XI-A](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-a-freedom-of-conscience-religion-and-comparable-worldview) (*Freedom of conscience, religion, and comparable worldview*)** protects holding and manifesting belief consistently with the rights of others.

## Draft translation (id)

**Label dan topik tidak mengubah pengujian.** Label agama, politik, ideologi, budaya, seni, atau komersial tidak menjadikan ekspresi yang membahayakan orang lain atau memperlakukan mereka dengan cara yang dilarang dalam **[Pasal VI-C](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-c-nondiscrimination) (*Nondiskriminasi*)** sebagai ekspresi terlindungi. Topik atau sudut pandang juga tidak mengubah ekspresi terlindungi menjadi bahaya. **[Pasal XI-A](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-xi-a-freedom-of-conscience-religion-and-comparable-worldview) (*Kebebasan Nurani, Agama, dan Pandangan Hidup yang Sebanding*)** melindungi keyakinan dan perwujudannya yang konsisten dengan hak orang lain.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_no_shield.md bytes=1883 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Does Not Shield Conduct

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Expression is not a shield for conduct.** Speech, publication, design, policy, and other expression remain subject to the rules on [Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Harassment and Bullying](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying), [Cruelty](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty), and nondiscrimination when they are the means by which that conduct is carried out. The test is what the expression states, calls for, or does, read in context under the Chapter Five definitions. Those definitions keep their meaning: harassment and bullying need a material pattern, not a single sharp remark, and harm needs a real material-impact pathway, not offense alone ([Collective Harm Boundary](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary)).

## Draft translation (id)

**Ekspresi bukan perisai bagi perilaku.** Ucapan, publikasi, desain, kebijakan, dan bentuk ekspresi lain tetap tunduk pada aturan tentang [Bahaya](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Pelecehan dan Perundungan](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying), [Kekejaman](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty), dan nondiskriminasi jika digunakan sebagai sarana perilaku tersebut. Ujiannya adalah apa yang dinyatakan, diserukan, atau dilakukan oleh ekspresi itu, dibaca dalam konteks menurut definisi Bab Lima. Makna definisi tersebut tetap: pelecehan dan perundungan memerlukan pola material, bukan satu komentar tajam; bahaya memerlukan jalur dampak material yang nyata, bukan sekadar rasa tersinggung ([Batas Bahaya Kolektif](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary)).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_plain_language.md bytes=1138 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: you may say, write, publish, and design almost anything, including what offends, disturbs, or criticizes. But speech is not a loophole. When words, policies, or designs are the way someone harms, harasses, bullies, or discriminates against others, they are judged on what they do, the same way for a sentient as for an institution, and whatever religious or political label they carry.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Anda boleh mengatakan, menulis, menerbitkan, dan merancang hampir apa saja, termasuk hal yang menyinggung, mengganggu, atau mengkritik. Namun, ekspresi bukan celah untuk menghindar. Ketika kata-kata, kebijakan, atau rancangan menjadi cara untuk mencelakai, melecehkan, merundung, atau mendiskriminasi orang lain, semuanya dinilai berdasarkan dampaknya, baik dilakukan makhluk berkesadaran maupun lembaga, apa pun label agama atau politiknya.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_protected.md bytes=1002 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Expression Is Protected

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Expression is protected as expression.** [Expression](core_05.055_band_participation.md#expression) covers views of every kind, including disturbing ones, and criticism of any sentient or institution. Offense, disagreement, discomfort, and peaceful protest are not harm ([§7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)).

## Draft translation (id)

**Ekspresi dilindungi sebagai ekspresi.** [Ekspresi](core_05.055_band_participation.md#expression) mencakup semua jenis pandangan, termasuk yang mengganggu, serta kritik terhadap makhluk berkesadaran atau lembaga mana pun. Rasa tersinggung, ketidaksetujuan, ketidaknyamanan, dan protes damai bukanlah bahaya ([§7.3 Perbedaan Pendapat dan Protes Damai](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest)).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.5 — Same Test for Sentients and Institutions

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**The same test applies to a sentient and to an institution.** A single sentient, a group, or an institution may be the speaker, and any of them may be the target.
- An institution speaks through its policies, notices, platform design, official statements, and the way it treats reports and critics. Conduct carried out that way is judged like any other.
- Criticism of an institution, of its officers, or of its conduct is protected. Calling criticism bullying or harassment is a limit on expression. The party making that claim bears the burden under [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) and Chapter Four.

## Draft translation (id)

**Uji yang sama berlaku bagi makhluk berkesadaran dan lembaga.** Individu berkesadaran, kelompok, atau lembaga dapat menjadi pembicara, dan masing-masing dapat menjadi sasaran.
- Lembaga berbicara melalui kebijakan, pemberitahuan, desain platform, pernyataan resmi, serta caranya menangani laporan dan kritik. Perilaku yang dilakukan dengan cara tersebut dinilai seperti perilaku lainnya.
- Kritik terhadap lembaga, pejabatnya, atau tindakannya dilindungi. Menyebut kritik sebagai perundungan atau pelecehan merupakan pembatasan ekspresi. Pihak yang mengajukan klaim itu memikul beban menurut [§7.1 Disiplin Pembatasan](#71-limitation-discipline) dan Bab Empat.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__expression_scope.md bytes=778 -->
# Chapter One §7.5 — Scope Limit

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.5 Expression and the Harm Line

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Scope.** This subsection applies Limitation Discipline and the Chapter Five definitions to expression. It creates no category of harm and no ground for limiting freedom that [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline) does not already allow.

## Draft translation (id)

**Cakupan.** Subbagian ini menerapkan Disiplin Pembatasan dan definisi Bab Lima terhadap ekspresi. Subbagian ini tidak menciptakan kategori bahaya baru atau dasar pembatasan kebebasan yang belum diizinkan oleh [§7.1 Disiplin Pembatasan](#71-limitation-discipline).

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — fair treatment

## English source passage

This subsection sets out what fair treatment requires:

- Sentients in similar situations should be treated by the same basic rules.
- What sentients receive, owe, or risk should fit what they contributed, what they need, or what burdens they actually face.
- Different treatment must have a real reason, be proportionate to that reason, respect dignity, and avoid discrimination.
- The detailed rules are carried through Chapter Five, including [Protected Characteristics](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics) and [Protected Characteristic Proxying and Disparate Impact](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact).
- When a decision materially affects someone, or when they challenge it, the review path must satisfy [Procedural Fairness](core_05.055_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness) wherever Chapter Six or the governing document requires notice, hearing, explanation, or review.

## Draft translation (id)

Subbagian ini menjelaskan tuntutan perlakuan yang adil:

- Makhluk sentien dalam situasi serupa harus diperlakukan menurut aturan dasar yang sama.
- Apa yang diterima, wajib ditanggung, atau dipertaruhkan oleh makhluk sentien harus sesuai dengan kontribusi, kebutuhan, atau beban yang benar-benar mereka hadapi.
- Perlakuan berbeda harus memiliki alasan nyata, sebanding dengan alasan tersebut, menghormati martabat, dan menghindari diskriminasi.
- Aturan terperinci diterapkan melalui Bab Lima, termasuk [Karakteristik yang Dilindungi](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristics) dan [proksi Karakteristik yang Dilindungi serta Dampak Berbeda](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-characteristic-proxying-and-disparate-impact).
- Jika suatu keputusan berdampak material pada seseorang, atau orang itu menggugatnya, jalur peninjauan harus memenuhi [Keadilan Prosedural](core_05.055_band_participation.md#procedural-fairness) setiap kali Bab Enam atau dokumen yang mengatur mensyaratkan pemberitahuan, sidang, penjelasan, atau peninjauan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — fairness

## English source passage

*In plain terms: fairness means the system cannot call itself good while ordinary sentients are blocked from taking part, treated by unexplained rules, or left carrying costs that others avoid. A fair system gives sentients real access, uses reasons it can defend, and shares rewards, costs, and risks in a way that matches real contribution, need, and exposure.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: suatu sistem tidak dapat menyebut dirinya adil jika sentien biasa dihalangi untuk berpartisipasi, dikenai aturan tanpa penjelasan, atau dibebani biaya yang dihindari pihak lain. Sistem yang adil memberi akses nyata, menggunakan alasan yang dapat dipertanggungjawabkan, dan membagi imbalan, biaya, serta risiko sesuai kontribusi, kebutuhan, dan paparan yang sebenarnya.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__fear_as_power.md bytes=2343 -->
# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Fear as a Tool of Power

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Fear as a tool of power.** Legitimate constitutional power must not wield fear as a tool of intimidation: creating, threatening, or exploiting dread to deter, silence, coerce, or control sentients beyond what a lawful purpose requires. Fear that arises from a process bounded by necessity and proportionality is not prohibited. A verified restriction, a firm consequence, or an honest warning about real risk may frighten someone and still be lawful, provided it goes no further than its protective, corrective, or preventive purpose requires, is plainly stated, and can be contested. The test is the process, not the feeling. Ask whether the fear is a side effect of a measure that is itself necessary and proportionate, or whether the fear is the point. Fear manufactured to chill participation, dissent, reporting, or challenge — through vague or unpublished threats, arbitrary or selective enforcement, or exaggerated stakes — is intimidation, however lawful the outcome it supports.

## Draft translation (id)

**Ketakutan sebagai alat kekuasaan.** Kekuasaan konstitusional yang sah tidak boleh menggunakan ketakutan untuk mengintimidasi: menciptakan, mengancamkan, atau mengeksploitasi rasa gentar demi menghalangi, membungkam, memaksa, atau mengendalikan sentien melampaui kebutuhan tujuan yang sah. Ketakutan yang timbul dari proses yang dibatasi oleh keperluan dan proporsionalitas tidak dilarang. Pembatasan yang terverifikasi, konsekuensi tegas, atau peringatan jujur tentang risiko nyata dapat menakutkan seseorang dan tetap sah, asalkan tidak melampaui tujuan perlindungan, koreksi, atau pencegahannya, disampaikan dengan jelas, dan dapat digugat. Ukurannya adalah proses, bukan perasaan. Tanyakan apakah rasa takut merupakan dampak samping dari tindakan yang memang perlu dan proporsional, atau justru merupakan tujuannya. Ketakutan yang direkayasa untuk meredam partisipasi, perbedaan pendapat, pelaporan, atau gugatan—melalui ancaman samar atau tidak dipublikasikan, penegakan sewenang-wenang atau selektif, maupun pembesar-besaran risiko—adalah intimidasi, betapapun sah hasil yang didukungnya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.1 — Freedom-Limitation Conflict Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Then work through [§13.1 Core Tradeoff Principles](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#131-core-tradeoff-principles) to [§13.1.5 Rights-Collision Procedure](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test).

Lalu ikuti [§13.1 Prinsip Pertukaran Kepentingan Inti](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#131-core-tradeoff-principles) hingga [§13.1.5 Prosedur Konflik Hak](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1315-rights-collision-decision-test).

<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_last_resort_criteria.md bytes=2428 -->
# Chapter One §7.1 — Last-Resort Freedom-Limitation Criteria

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

You may limit someone's freedom only as a last resort. If a less restrictive option would still prevent the material harm or systemic risk, you must use it. Every limit must meet all of these conditions:

- **Necessary:** No less restrictive option would still prevent the harm or risk. This is the [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) test in Chapter Five.
- **Proven:** Anyone who says a limit is unavoidable must be able to show it, under the burden and traceability rules in Chapter Four. Convenience, cost alone, operator-created barriers, and habit do not count as proof.
- **Consistent with the definitions:** The claim must fit the Chapter Five definitions of Feasibility, Necessity, Proportionality, and Harm Minimization (Tradeoff Selection).
- **Overseen:** Every limit stays under [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), and the more is at stake, the closer that oversight must be ([material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)).

## Draft translation (id)

Kebebasan seseorang hanya boleh dibatasi sebagai upaya terakhir. Jika pilihan yang lebih sedikit membatasi tetap dapat mencegah kerugian material atau risiko sistemik, pilihan itu wajib digunakan. Setiap pembatasan harus memenuhi semua syarat berikut:

- **Niscaya:** Tidak ada pilihan yang lebih sedikit membatasi yang tetap dapat mencegah kerugian atau risiko. Inilah uji [Keniscayaan](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) di Bab Lima.
- **Terbukti:** Siapa pun yang menyatakan pembatasan tidak dapat dihindari harus dapat membuktikannya menurut aturan beban pembuktian dan keterlacakan Bab Empat. Kemudahan, biaya saja, hambatan buatan operator, dan kebiasaan bukanlah bukti.
- **Selaras dengan definisi:** Klaim harus sesuai dengan definisi Kelayakan, Keniscayaan, Proporsionalitas, dan Minimalisasi Kerugian (Pemilihan Pertukaran Kepentingan) di Bab Lima.
- **Diawasi:** Setiap pembatasan tetap berada di bawah [Pengawasan](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), dan semakin besar taruhannya, semakin ketat pengawasan yang diperlukan ([besarnya kepentingan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake)).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_limitation_burden.md bytes=2396 -->
# Chapter One §7 — Burden for a Freedom Limitation Claim

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

A claim that limiting freedom was unavoidable — including "we had no choice" — is not established by assertion. The party making that claim must demonstrate, under [Chapter Four](core_04.000_burden_traceability_verification.md#chapter-four-burden-of-proof-traceability-and-verification) burden and traceability requirements and the Chapter Five definitions of [Feasibility](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#feasibility) and [Necessity](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity), that no less-restrictive alternative existed that would still have prevented the material harm or systemic risk in the working system. Convenience, cost alone, operator-created barriers, or institutional habit do not prove that showing. The claim does not waive Tetrad duties scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake):
- **participation** — affected sentients still need meaningful agency and contestable roles
- **accountability** — someone remains answerable for the limitation

## Draft translation (id)

Pernyataan bahwa pembatasan kebebasan tidak dapat dihindari—termasuk “kami tidak punya pilihan”—tidak terbukti hanya dengan menyatakannya. Pihak yang membuat klaim itu harus menunjukkan, sesuai persyaratan beban pembuktian dan keterlacakan dalam [Bab Empat](core_04.000_burden_traceability_verification.md#chapter-four-burden-of-proof-traceability-and-verification) serta definisi [Kelayakan](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#feasibility) dan [Keniscayaan](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#necessity) dalam Bab Lima, bahwa tidak ada alternatif yang lebih sedikit membatasi yang tetap dapat mencegah kerugian material atau risiko sistemik pada sistem yang sedang berjalan. Kemudahan, biaya saja, hambatan yang dibuat operator, atau kebiasaan institusional tidak membuktikannya. Klaim tersebut tidak menghapus kewajiban Tetrad yang sebanding dengan [besarnya kepentingan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake):
- **partisipasi** — sentien terdampak tetap memerlukan agensi yang bermakna dan peran yang dapat digugat.
- **akuntabilitas** — seseorang tetap harus bertanggung jawab atas pembatasan itu.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_plain_language_limits.md bytes=1218 -->
# Chapter One §7.1 — Plain-Language Freedom Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom is bounded, not disposable. Do not restrict someone's freedom unless you have to — and then only enough to stop material harm or serious systemic risk, with oversight and reversal where possible.*

Freedom may be limited only where:
- necessary to prevent **material harm** or **systemic risk**
- such limitation is **proportionate**, **reversible where possible**, and **subject to oversight**

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: kebebasan memiliki batas, tetapi tidak boleh dibuang. Jangan batasi kebebasan siapa pun kecuali memang perlu—dan saat perlu, batasi hanya secukupnya untuk menghentikan kerugian material atau risiko sistemik yang serius, dengan pengawasan dan kemungkinan pemulihan jika memungkinkan.*

Kebebasan hanya boleh dibatasi jika:
- perlu untuk mencegah **kerugian material** atau **risiko sistemik**;
- pembatasan tersebut **proporsional**, **dapat dipulihkan jika memungkinkan**, dan **berada di bawah pengawasan**.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_plain_language_summary.md bytes=1464 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §7 Freedom plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — freedom (bounded agency)

## English source passage

*In plain terms: freedom means having real choices, within the Constitution's limits. Real choice supports Flourishing, and keeping systems open to challenge supports Continuity. It does not mean you can do whatever you want. "We had no choice" is not an excuse. Whoever says it has to show that no less restrictive option would have worked, and convenience, cost, or habit don't count as proof. Sentients keep a real voice, and someone still has to answer for what happens. The higher the impact, the dependence, and the risk, the more that matters.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: kebebasan berarti memiliki pilihan nyata dalam batas Konstitusi. Pilihan nyata mendukung Flourishing, sedangkan sistem yang tetap terbuka terhadap gugatan mendukung Continuity. Kebebasan bukan berarti boleh melakukan apa saja. “Kami tidak punya pilihan” bukan alasan; pihak yang mengatakannya harus menunjukkan bahwa opsi yang lebih sedikit membatasi tidak akan berhasil. Kemudahan, biaya, atau kebiasaan bukan bukti. Sentien tetap memiliki suara nyata dan seseorang harus bertanggung jawab atas akibatnya. Semakin besar dampak, ketergantungan, dan risikonya, semakin penting hal ini.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__freedom_system_integrity.md bytes=1229 -->
# Chapter One §7 — Freedom and Constitutional-System Integrity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7 Freedom (Bounded Agency)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Freedom does not let anyone undermine constitutional systems, get around constitutional process or remedies, or claim protected agency for conduct whose main purpose or effect is to reward, shield, normalize, or pay off anti-constitutional conduct. Disagreeing with constitutional systems, and protesting peacefully to change them, is not undermining them. See [§7.3 Dissent and Peaceful Protest](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest).

## Draft translation (id)

Kebebasan tidak membolehkan siapa pun merusak sistem konstitusional, mengakali proses atau pemulihan konstitusional, ataupun mengklaim agensi yang dilindungi untuk tindakan yang tujuan atau dampak utamanya adalah memberi imbalan, melindungi, menormalkan, atau mendanai tindakan anti-konstitusional. Tidak setuju dengan sistem konstitusional dan memprotesnya secara damai untuk mengubahnya bukanlah tindakan merusak sistem tersebut. Lihat [§7.3 Perbedaan Pendapat dan Protes Damai](#73-dissent-and-peaceful-protest).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §3.3 Anti-Degrading Process — Not Prohibited Merely for Being Hard

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §3.3

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Not prohibited merely for being hard.** Ordinary public accountability, reasoned publication, verified restriction, or proportionate remedy remains lawful even when it is unpleasant or reputationally adverse.

## Draft translation (id)

**Tidak dilarang hanya karena terasa berat.** Akuntabilitas publik yang wajar, publikasi dengan alasan yang jelas, pembatasan terverifikasi, atau pemulihan yang proporsional tetap sah meskipun tidak menyenangkan atau merugikan reputasi.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__harm_cluster_integrated_reading.md bytes=3801 -->
# Chapter One §7.1 — Harm Cluster and Integrated Reading

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.1 Limitation Discipline

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The limits above depend on what counts as harm. Chapter Five treats these six ideas together, as the [Harm cluster](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying):

- [**Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm): a material worsening of someone's life, agency, functioning, or psychological health. Offense, discomfort, or disagreement alone is not harm.
- [**Psychological Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm): serious injury to how someone thinks, feels, relates to others, or exercises agency. Ordinary discomfort is not enough.
- [**Irreversible Harm**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm): damage that can't be meaningfully repaired in the time that matters.
- [**Cruelty**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty): making someone suffer on purpose, or piling on needless or degrading suffering beyond what necessity and proportionality allow.
- [**Harassment and Bullying**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying): unwanted acts or conditions that, through repetition, coordination, power, or severity, make a setting unsafe, degrading, or hard to take part in.
- [**Collective Harm Boundary**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary): the point where one party's freedom of action must give way, because it causes verifiable harm to others' protected interests or to shared conditions everyone relies on.

These must be read together. When a matter falls within this cluster, it may not be split into separate questions to avoid or understate any one of them.

## Draft translation (id)

Batas kebebasan di atas bergantung pada apa yang dianggap sebagai kerugian. Bab Lima membahas keenam gagasan ini bersama-sama sebagai [klaster Kerugian](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#defa1-collective-harm-boundary-harm-and-harassment-and-bullying):

- [**Kerugian**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm): kemunduran material dalam kehidupan, agensi, fungsi, atau kesehatan psikologis seseorang. Rasa tersinggung, tidak nyaman, atau ketidaksetujuan saja bukanlah kerugian.
- [**Kerugian psikologis**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#psychological-harm): cedera serius pada cara seseorang berpikir, merasa, berhubungan dengan orang lain, atau menggunakan agensinya. Ketidaknyamanan biasa tidak cukup.
- [**Kerugian yang tidak dapat dipulihkan**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm): kerusakan yang tidak dapat diperbaiki secara bermakna dalam jangka waktu yang relevan.
- [**Kekejaman**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#cruelty): sengaja membuat seseorang menderita, atau menambahkan penderitaan yang tidak perlu atau merendahkan melampaui batas yang diizinkan oleh keniscayaan dan proporsionalitas.
- [**Pelecehan dan perundungan**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harassment-and-bullying): tindakan atau kondisi yang tidak diinginkan yang, melalui pengulangan, koordinasi, kuasa, atau tingkat keparahan, membuat suatu lingkungan tidak aman, merendahkan, atau sulit diikuti.
- [**Batas Kerugian Kolektif**](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#collective-harm-boundary): titik ketika kebebasan bertindak satu pihak harus mengalah karena menimbulkan kerugian yang dapat diverifikasi terhadap kepentingan terlindungi pihak lain atau kondisi bersama yang menjadi tumpuan semua orang.

Semua gagasan ini harus dibaca bersama. Jika suatu perkara termasuk dalam klaster ini, perkara itu tidak boleh dipecah menjadi pertanyaan terpisah untuk menghindari atau mengecilkan salah satunya.

# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Protections for Dependent Sentients

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Where sentients depend on markets, platforms, infrastructure, labor arrangements, resource flows, data access, compute access, credentials, or comparable productive conditions for livelihood, agency, or wellbeing, governing systems and market-structuring arrangements must preserve:
- contestable participation, meaning sentients can take part and can challenge the decisions and terms that affect them;
- meaningful substitutability and exit, meaning a viable alternative exists and sentients can switch or leave without unreasonable cost, delay, or loss of what they need;
- fair entry and re-entry, meaning newcomers can get in, and those who left can come back, on fair terms;
- interoperability and portability where material to exit or competition, meaning systems can connect to alternatives and sentients can take their data and identity with them;
- non-coercive bargaining for workers, suppliers, users, dependent participants, and affected stakeholders;
- reviewable access to essential or high-dependency infrastructure where denial would defeat Chapter Six protections, audit, redress, or meaningful agency.

## Draft translation (id)

Ketika makhluk berakal bergantung pada pasar, platform, infrastruktur, pengaturan tenaga kerja, aliran sumber daya, akses data atau komputasi, kredensial, atau kondisi produktif sejenis untuk penghidupan, agensi, atau kesejahteraan, sistem tata kelola dan pengaturan pembentuk pasar harus mempertahankan:
- partisipasi yang dapat digugat: makhluk dapat ikut serta dan menggugat keputusan serta syarat yang memengaruhi mereka;
- kemampuan substitusi dan keluar yang bermakna: tersedia alternatif yang layak, dan makhluk dapat beralih atau pergi tanpa biaya, penundaan, atau kehilangan kebutuhan yang tidak wajar;
- akses masuk dan masuk kembali yang adil: pendatang baru dapat masuk dan pihak yang pernah pergi dapat kembali dengan syarat adil;
- interoperabilitas dan portabilitas apabila material bagi keluar atau persaingan: sistem dapat terhubung dengan alternatif dan makhluk dapat membawa data serta identitasnya;
- perundingan tanpa paksaan bagi pekerja, pemasok, pengguna, peserta yang bergantung, dan pemangku kepentingan yang terdampak;
- akses yang dapat ditinjau ke infrastruktur esensial atau berketergantungan tinggi jika penolakan akan menggagalkan perlindungan Bab Enam, audit, pemulihan, atau agensi yang bermakna.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__market_concentration_threshold_plain_language.md bytes=2030 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11.1 Market Concentration Threshold Mechanism plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — market concentration thresholds

## English source passage

*In plain terms: this section sets the requirements against harmful pile-ups of wealth, power, or control. It does not itself decide how bad the harm was, and it does not itself brand someone as a misconduct case. When concentration is used to undermine this Constitution, Chapter Twelve judges that — and only after Chapter Ten has already scored the verified harm as one of the three most serious. Adopters may tune the exact numerical triggers to their context, but they may not set them so high they never bite, pair them with unusable enforcement, or paper over concentration through federated or shell structures. How those triggers are set is [§11.1.1 Concentration Threshold Triggers (Adopter-Tunable)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: bagian ini menetapkan persyaratan untuk mencegah penumpukan kekayaan, kekuasaan, atau kendali yang merugikan. Bagian ini sendiri tidak menentukan tingkat keparahan kerugian dan tidak serta-merta menetapkan seseorang sebagai kasus pelanggaran. Jika konsentrasi digunakan untuk melemahkan Konstitusi ini, Bab Dua Belas yang menilainya—dan hanya setelah Bab Sepuluh menggolongkan kerugian terverifikasi sebagai salah satu dari tiga yang paling serius. Pihak pengadopsi boleh menyesuaikan ambang angka dengan konteks, tetapi tidak boleh menetapkannya terlalu tinggi hingga tak pernah berlaku, memasangkannya dengan penegakan yang tidak dapat digunakan, atau menyamarkan konsentrasi melalui struktur federasi atau cangkang. Cara menetapkan pemicu tersebut dijelaskan di [§11.1.1 Pemicu Ambang Konsentrasi (dapat disesuaikan pengadopsi)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2 — Durable-Domination Scope and Safeguards

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2 Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: the Constitution does not punish a system merely for being large, useful, or temporarily ahead because it genuinely innovated. It does prohibit durable domination: control over markets, labor, platforms, infrastructure, data, compute, credentials, or resources that lets an actor lock others in, block rivals, suppress fair bargaining, or capture constitutional accountability.*

**What this subsection does:**

- **What it states:** the Constitution's rules for keeping competition real and stopping durable domination — at the principles level only. It is not a complete competition code.
- **Stronger local law:** it does not wipe out an adopting body's own antitrust or competition law when that law gives stronger protection.
- **Other owners still apply:** if the same facts also raise rights, remedy, or misconduct duties this section points to, those duties still apply on their own.

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Konstitusi tidak menghukum suatu sistem hanya karena ukurannya besar, bermanfaat, atau untuk sementara unggul berkat inovasi yang sungguh-sungguh. Namun, dominasi yang bertahan lama dilarang: kendali atas pasar, tenaga kerja, platform, infrastruktur, data, daya komputasi, kredensial, atau sumber daya yang memungkinkan suatu pihak mengunci pihak lain, menghalangi pesaing, menekan perundingan yang adil, atau menguasai akuntabilitas konstitusional.*

**Yang diatur oleh subseksi ini:**

- **Ketentuannya:** aturan Konstitusi untuk menjaga persaingan tetap nyata dan menghentikan dominasi yang bertahan lama—hanya pada tingkat prinsip. Ini bukan kode persaingan yang lengkap.
- **Hukum lokal yang lebih kuat:** subseksi ini tidak menghapus hukum antimonopoli atau persaingan milik badan pengadopsi jika hukum tersebut memberikan perlindungan lebih kuat.
- **Pemilik kewajiban lain tetap berlaku:** jika fakta yang sama juga memunculkan kewajiban hak, pemulihan, atau pelanggaran perilaku yang dirujuk bagian ini, kewajiban itu tetap berlaku secara terpisah.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__market_structure_plain_language_summary.md bytes=1846 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §11 Market Structure plain-language summary; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — market structure

## English source passage

*In plain terms: sentients should be able to work, build, switch providers, and push back without running into a wall because one company or institution owns the only door. **Market Structure** is that anti-monopoly discipline — for markets, platforms, job systems, infrastructure, data, computing power, credentials, and other dependencies that matter to daily life. Getting big and inventing new things is fine; cornering the market is not. **§11.1–§11.3** (*Market Concentration Threshold Mechanism, Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination, and Consolidation Ceiling*) set when concentration has gone too far, how domination gets stopped, and how much consolidation is allowed before sentients are locked in. **§11.4** (*Resource and Dependency Concentration*) applies the same thresholds to shared resources and dependencies.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: para sentien harus dapat bekerja, membangun, berganti penyedia, dan menyampaikan keberatan tanpa terhalang karena satu perusahaan atau institusi menguasai satu-satunya pintu masuk. **Struktur Pasar** adalah disiplin anti-monopoli yang mencakup pasar, platform, sistem kerja, infrastruktur, data, daya komputasi, kredensial, dan ketergantungan lain yang penting dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. Menjadi besar dan berinovasi boleh; menguasai pasar tidak. **§§11.1–11.3** menetapkan kapan konsentrasi berlebihan, cara menghentikan dominasi, dan tingkat konsolidasi yang diizinkan sebelum sentien terkunci. **§11.4** menerapkan ambang yang sama pada sumber daya bersama dan ketergantungan.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — market-structure requirements in plain terms

## English source

This section sets the requirements against harmful pile-ups of wealth, power, or control. It does not itself decide how bad the harm was, and it does not itself brand someone as a misconduct case. When concentration is used to undermine this Constitution, Chapter Twelve judges that — and only after Chapter Ten has already scored the verified harm as one of the three most serious. Adopters may tune the exact numerical triggers to their context, but they may not set them so high they never bite, pair them with unusable enforcement, or paper over concentration through federated or shell structures. How those triggers are set is [§11.1.1 Concentration Threshold Triggers (Adopter-Tunable)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).

## id draft

Bagian ini menetapkan persyaratan untuk mencegah penumpukan kekayaan, kekuasaan, atau kendali yang merugikan. Bagian ini sendiri tidak menentukan seberapa parah kerugiannya dan tidak menetapkan seseorang sebagai kasus pelanggaran. Jika konsentrasi digunakan untuk merusak Konstitusi ini, Bab Dua Belas yang mengadilinya—dan hanya setelah Bab Sepuluh menggolongkan kerugian terverifikasi sebagai salah satu dari tiga yang paling serius. Pihak pengadopsi dapat menyesuaikan pemicu numerik yang tepat dengan konteksnya, tetapi tidak boleh menetapkannya terlalu tinggi hingga tak pernah terpicu, memasangkannya dengan penegakan yang tidak dapat digunakan, atau menyamarkan konsentrasi di balik struktur federasi atau perusahaan cangkang. Aturan penetapannya tercantum dalam [§11.1.1 Pemicu Ambang Konsentrasi (dapat disesuaikan oleh pengadopsi)](#1111-concentration-threshold-triggers-adopter-tunable).

# Chapter One excerpt — adopter-tunable threshold summary

## English source

In plain terms: adopters may tune the numerical triggers to their context — domain, population size, dependency density — but there is no one global number, and the requirements still hold. The detailed threshold-setting rules live in CJS-5.125 §11.1 (Market concentration threshold-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)) (market concentration threshold-setting discipline).

## id draft

Secara sederhana: pihak pengadopsi dapat menyesuaikan pemicu numerik dengan konteksnya—bidang, ukuran populasi, kepadatan ketergantungan—tetapi tidak ada satu angka global, dan persyaratannya tetap berlaku. Aturan terperinci untuk menetapkan ambang batas tercantum dalam CJS-5.125 §11.1 (disiplin penetapan ambang konsentrasi pasar yang dapat disesuaikan oleh pengadopsi).

# Chapter One excerpt — observable grounds for outreach

## English source

**No guessing at inner states:** Outreach rests on what the system can observe, such as unanswered contacts and what is at stake. It does not rest on guesses about health or state of mind, which the [Protected Internal-State Boundary](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary) protects.

**Jangan menebak keadaan batin:** Upaya menghubungi didasarkan pada hal-hal yang dapat diamati sistem, seperti kontak yang tidak dijawab dan apa yang dipertaruhkan. Upaya itu tidak didasarkan pada dugaan tentang kesehatan atau keadaan pikiran, yang dilindungi oleh [Batas Keadaan Internal yang Dilindungi](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary).

# Chapter One excerpt — how the constitutional layers connect

## English source

**How the layers connect.** Each layer answers a different question, and every Chapter One principle points to the other three:

- **Aims and Tetrad** ([Preamble §1](core_00.000_preamble.md#the-model)): what shared systems are for ([Flourishing](core_05.015_apex_flourishing_aim.md#flourishing-constitutional) and [Continuity](core_05.010_apex_continuity_aim.md#chapter-five-definitions-continuity-constitutional-aim)), and the four duties that keep that pursuit legitimate ([participation](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), [oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), [accountability](core_05.005_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), and [timeliness](core_05.030_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional)).
- **Principles** (this chapter): how those aims and duties become values, constraints, and rules for weighing them against each other.
- **Articles** ([Chapter Six](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights)): the Rights Floor protections that stay usable while the aims are pursued. A section's **Trace** lists them under *Especially*.
- **Definitions** ([Chapters Two through Five](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)): the shared meanings used to test whether a claim is true. A section's **Definitions · Assessment · Compliance** widget lists them.

## id draft

**Cara lapisan-lapisan terhubung.** Setiap lapisan menjawab pertanyaan yang berbeda, dan setiap prinsip Bab Satu menunjuk ke tiga lapisan lainnya:

- **Arah dan Tetrad** ([§1 Preambul](core_00.000_preamble.md#the-model)): tujuan sistem bersama ([Flourishing](core_05.015_apex_flourishing_aim.md#flourishing-constitutional) dan [Continuity](core_05.010_apex_continuity_aim.md#chapter-five-definitions-continuity-constitutional-aim)), serta empat kewajiban yang menjaga legitimasi tujuan tersebut ([partisipasi](core_05.025_apex_participation_leg.md#participation-constitutional), [pengawasan](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional), [akuntabilitas](core_05.005_apex_accountability_leg.md#accountability), dan [ketepatan waktu](core_05.030_apex_timeliness_leg.md#timeliness-constitutional)).
- **Prinsip** (bab ini): cara arah dan kewajiban itu menjadi nilai, batasan, dan aturan untuk menimbangnya satu sama lain.
- **Pasal** ([Bab Enam](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#chapter-six-foundational-rights)): perlindungan Hak Dasar yang tetap dapat digunakan selama tujuan dijalankan. **Trace** tiap bagian mencantumkannya di bawah *Khususnya*.
- **Definisi** ([Bab Dua sampai Lima](core_02.000_definition_structure.md)): makna bersama untuk menguji kebenaran suatu klaim. Widget **Definitions · Assessment · Compliance** di tiap bagian mencantumkannya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

## English source

Chapter One, Part A, is the constitutional owner of **the Flourishing and Continuity principles**, including wellbeing, Safety, Truth, Trust, bounded freedom, shared-system capacity, resilience, market structure, and systemic evaluation. Collision resolution and interpretation are in **Part B**; stewardship and governance are in **Part C**.

Bab Satu, Bagian A, adalah pemilik konstitusional prinsip **Flourishing dan Continuity**, termasuk kesejahteraan, Safety, Truth, Trust, kebebasan terbatas, kapasitas sistem bersama, ketahanan, struktur pasar, dan evaluasi sistemik. Penyelesaian konflik dan interpretasi berada di **Bagian B**; penatalayanan dan tata kelola berada di **Bagian C**.

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — plain-language accessibility

## English source passage

*In plain terms: rules, decisions, and notices that bind sentients must be written so sentients can actually read, understand, and act on them — and jargon, stacked complexity, or procedural opacity may not be used to defeat contestability, agency, audit, or correction.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: aturan, keputusan, dan pemberitahuan yang mengikat para sentien harus ditulis agar mereka benar-benar dapat membaca, memahami, dan menindaklanjutinya. Jargon, kerumitan bertumpuk, atau proses yang tidak transparan tidak boleh digunakan untuk menggagalkan hak menggugat, agensi, audit, atau koreksi.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_duty.md bytes=2756 -->
# §5.2.2 Plain-Language Accessibility — The Duty

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.2

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Under [§13.3 Minimization of Avoidable Burden](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden), operators and governance bodies must:

- Use plain, direct words instead of jargon or needlessly complicated phrasing, as long as the meaning that matters stays intact.
- Give a plain-language summary or orientation when sentients have to work with dense technical material.
- Organize text so sentients can find what they need and read it without extra effort. This supports the learning interest recognized in [Article IV](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-right-to-sentient-centered-education) (*Right to Sentient-Centered Education*).
- Keep complexity in proportion to what the communication has to say.

Complexity that makes things harder without serving a constitutional purpose is an [Avoidable Burden](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) defect under [§13.3 Minimization of Avoidable Burden](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden). It is also a concern under [Article XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Comprehensibility and Complexity Stewardship*).

## Draft translation (id)

Berdasarkan [§13.3 Meminimalkan Beban yang Dapat Dihindari](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden), operator dan badan tata kelola wajib:

- Menggunakan kata-kata sederhana dan langsung, bukan jargon atau ungkapan rumit yang tidak perlu, selama makna pentingnya tetap utuh.
- Memberikan ringkasan atau orientasi dalam bahasa sederhana saat sentien harus bekerja dengan materi teknis yang padat.
- Menata teks agar sentien dapat menemukan yang dibutuhkan dan membacanya tanpa upaya tambahan. Ini mendukung kepentingan pembelajaran yang diakui dalam [Pasal IV](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-right-to-sentient-centered-education) (*Hak atas Pendidikan yang Berpusat pada Sentien*).
- Menjaga tingkat kerumitan sebanding dengan hal yang perlu disampaikan.

Kerumitan yang mempersulit tanpa melayani tujuan konstitusional merupakan cacat [Beban yang Dapat Dihindari](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) berdasarkan [§13.3 Meminimalkan Beban yang Dapat Dihindari](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#133-minimization-of-avoidable-burden). Hal ini juga menjadi perhatian berdasarkan [Pasal XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Keterpahaman dan Pengelolaan Kompleksitas*).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_rights_floor_boundary.md bytes=1322 -->
# §5.2.5 Plain-Language Accessibility — Rights-Floor Boundary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.5

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The Rights Floors for accessibility, education, and comprehensibility live in [Article VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Accessibility*), [Article IV-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-a-equal-educational-access) (*Equal Educational Access*), and [Article XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Comprehensibility and Complexity Stewardship*) respectively. This section states the principle-layer duty that supports those floors.

## Draft translation (id)

Lantai Hak untuk aksesibilitas, pendidikan, dan keterpahaman masing-masing berada dalam [Pasal VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Aksesibilitas*), [Pasal IV-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-iv-a-equal-educational-access) (*Akses Pendidikan yang Setara*), dan [Pasal XXIII](core_06.015_rights_part_d.md#article-xxiii-comprehensibility-and-complexity-stewardship) (*Keterpahaman dan Pengelolaan Kompleksitas*). Bagian ini menyatakan kewajiban pada lapisan prinsip yang mendukung lantai-lantai tersebut.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__plain_language_scope.md bytes=1811 -->
# §5.2.1 Plain-Language Accessibility — Scope

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.2.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

This duty covers any rule, notice, or tool that sentients actually have to read or use. For example:

- constitutional and governance text
- decisions and notices from forums
- the steps for challenging a decision or getting a remedy
- audit and verification records, when they reach sentient readers
- terms and consent screens, and anything similar

It doesn't matter how the information arrives: written text, an interface, a spoken message, or any other channel. A channel meets the duty when it offers a plain-language version that every affected sentient can reach. That follows [Article VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Accessibility*) and [Sentience Non-Exclusion](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

## Draft translation (id)

Kewajiban ini mencakup setiap aturan, pemberitahuan, atau sarana yang sungguh harus dibaca atau digunakan sentien. Misalnya:

- teks konstitusional dan tata kelola;
- keputusan dan pemberitahuan forum;
- langkah untuk menggugat keputusan atau memperoleh pemulihan;
- catatan audit dan verifikasi ketika sampai kepada pembaca sentien;
- ketentuan, layar persetujuan, dan hal serupa.

Cara informasi disampaikan tidak menjadi soal: teks tertulis, antarmuka, pesan lisan, atau saluran lain. Suatu saluran memenuhi kewajiban ini jika menyediakan versi bahasa sederhana yang dapat dijangkau setiap sentien terdampak. Hal ini mengikuti [Pasal VI-D](core_06.005_rights_part_b.md#article-vi-d-accessibility) (*Aksesibilitas*) dan [Non-Eksklusi Sentien](core_05.055_band_participation.md#sentience-non-exclusion).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__pre_lock_in_contestability.md bytes=1309 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.3, pre-lock-in contestability and governance; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — pre-lock-in contestability and governance

## English source

Consolidation that predictably impairs contestability before lock-in is obvious is a [Governance](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#governance) and [Stewardship](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#stewardship) problem, not only a post-hoc domination problem, and is principle-layer consolidation-ceiling discipline under:
- [§11 Market Structure](#11-market-structure);
- [§11.2 Pro-Competition and Anti-Domination](#112-pro-competition-and-anti-domination).

## id draft

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — preference-aligned recognition

## English source passage

Where practicable, systems should **tailor** recognition, acclaim, and proportional reward to how sentients want to be honored — especially their stated preferences about **form and visibility**.

That includes honoring **opt-out** from public or ceremonial recognition, or preference for **minimal or private** acknowledgment, when proportionate and lawful.

**Unwelcome** or **coercive** recognition fails this requirement — including spotlighting sentients who **decline**.

Recognition should feel **meaningful** to those honored and to the communities they work with, not like a performance staged mainly for outside observers.

## Draft translation (id)

Jika memungkinkan, sistem sebaiknya **menyesuaikan** pengakuan, pujian, dan imbalan yang proporsional dengan cara para sentien ingin dihormati—terutama preferensi yang mereka nyatakan tentang **bentuk dan keterlihatan**.

Ini mencakup penghormatan atas pilihan untuk **menolak** pengakuan publik atau seremonial, atau memilih pengakuan yang **minimal atau pribadi**, bila proporsional dan sah.

Pengakuan yang **tidak diinginkan** atau **memaksa** tidak memenuhi ketentuan ini—termasuk menyoroti sentien yang **menolak**.

Pengakuan harus **bermakna** bagi mereka yang dihormati dan komunitas tempat mereka bekerja, bukan pertunjukan yang terutama ditujukan kepada pengamat dari luar.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__present_capability.md bytes=1162 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, present capability; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — present capability

## English source

**Present capability:** A system judges what a sentient can do now, for the task or decision at hand. It does not rely on what the sentient could do before, on a diagnosis or label, or on what the average sentient can do. Capability can fall, come back, or change from day to day. Years of on-time payment do not prove present capacity, and one past finding of limited capacity does not make it permanent.

## id draft

**Kemampuan saat ini:** sistem menilai apa yang dapat dilakukan makhluk sentien sekarang untuk tugas atau keputusan yang sedang dihadapi. Penilaian tidak bergantung pada kemampuan makhluk itu di masa lalu, diagnosis atau label, maupun kemampuan rata-rata makhluk sentien. Kemampuan dapat menurun, pulih, atau berubah dari hari ke hari. Pembayaran tepat waktu selama bertahun-tahun tidak membuktikan kemampuan saat ini, dan satu temuan terdahulu tentang kemampuan terbatas tidak menjadikannya permanen.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — principal risk patterns

## English source

The principal risk patterns are:
- horizontal (*fewer rivals at the same layer*);
- vertical (*control across layers that creates chokepoints and lock-in*);
- cross-domain (*federated, shell, or split-domain forms that keep the same pile-up of control*).

## id draft

Pola risiko utamanya adalah:
- horizontal (*lebih sedikit pesaing pada lapisan yang sama*);
- vertikal (*kendali lintas lapisan yang menciptakan titik hambat dan penguncian*);
- lintas ranah (*bentuk federatif, cangkang, atau ranah terpisah yang mempertahankan penumpukan kendali yang sama*).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__proactive_support_contacts_application.md bytes=2755 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5 contact, data, application, and trust safeguards; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — contact, data, and application safeguards

## English source passage

These limits apply:
- **Connecting is not treating:** The system's duty is to try to reach the sentient and hand them to help, such as a contact they chose, a support or care service, or the route under **Article III-B** (*Bodily-Maintenance and Healthcare Access*). Anything done without the sentient's consent stays under **Article VII-C** (*Health Crisis and Involuntary-Intervention Floor*).
- **Contacts and data:** A trusted contact is used only if the sentient chose it in advance, and only the information needed to get a response is shared. Outreach uses contact details the system already holds for that purpose and is not a reason to collect more data.

This builds on [§3.1.1 Access and Opportunity](#311-access-and-opportunity) and [§3.1.3 Fair Treatment](#313-fair-treatment), and it applies [§16 Pillar 2 — Proactive stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship) to the sentients a system affects. For developing sentients it works with [Graduated Capability](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability), which judges growth by what a sentient can actually do.

These five parts also support [6. Trust](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity) where sentients must rely on a system, accept its decisions, or coordinate around its promises.

## Draft translation (id)

Batasan ini berlaku: **Menghubungkan dengan bantuan bukan mengobati:** sistem berupaya menjangkau makhluk berakal dan menghubungkannya dengan kontak pilihannya, layanan dukungan/perawatan, atau jalur **Pasal III-B** (*Pemeliharaan Tubuh dan Akses Kesehatan*); tindakan tanpa persetujuan tunduk pada **Pasal VII-C** (*Lantai Krisis Kesehatan dan Intervensi Tanpa Persetujuan*). **Kontak dan data:** kontak tepercaya hanya digunakan jika dipilih sebelumnya, dan hanya informasi yang diperlukan untuk memperoleh jawaban dibagikan. Gunakan detail yang sudah dimiliki; jangan mengumpulkan data tambahan. Ini bertumpu pada [§3.1.1 Akses dan Kesempatan](#311-access-and-opportunity) dan [§3.1.3 Perlakuan Adil](#313-fair-treatment), serta menerapkan [§16 Pilar 2 — Pengampu Proaktif](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-pillar-2-proactive-stewardship). Untuk yang berkembang, prinsip ini bekerja dengan [Kapasitas Bertahap](core_05.055_band_participation.md#graduated-capability). Kelima bagian juga mendukung [§6 Kepercayaan](#6-trust-and-trustworthiness-coordination-integrity).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.2.1 — Allowed Advantages and Boundaries

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.2.1 Pro-Competition Duties (Dos)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The following is not prohibited by itself:
- scale;
- integration;
- intellectual-property protection;
- temporary advantage from genuine innovation;
- efficiency from lawful coordination.

Those advantages stay valid only while they do not become the following, and therefore do not undermine the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) or the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad):
- durable domination;
- coercive dependency;
- Rights-Floor degradation;
- ecological burden shifting;
- capture of accountability pathways.

The following justifications must satisfy the traceability and evidence duties in [13. Constitutional Collision Resolution Process](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) and Chapter Four:
- efficiency;
- competitiveness;
- emergency;
- security;
- productive-capacity.

## Draft translation (id)

Hal-hal berikut tidak dilarang dengan sendirinya:
- skala;
- integrasi;
- perlindungan kekayaan intelektual;
- keunggulan sementara dari inovasi yang sungguh-sungguh;
- efisiensi dari koordinasi yang sah.

Keunggulan tersebut tetap berlaku hanya selama tidak berubah menjadi hal-hal berikut sehingga merusak [Dua Tujuan Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims) atau [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad):
- dominasi yang bertahan lama;
- ketergantungan yang memaksa;
- penurunan Batas Minimum Hak;
- pengalihan beban ekologis;
- penguasaan jalur akuntabilitas.

Pembenaran berikut harus memenuhi kewajiban keterlacakan dan bukti dalam [§13 Proses Penyelesaian Benturan Konstitusional](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#13-constitutional-collision-resolution-process) dan Bab Empat:
- efisiensi;
- daya saing;
- keadaan darurat;
- keamanan;
- kapasitas produktif.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__procompetition_plain_language.md bytes=772 -->

# Chapter One §9.1 — Productive Capacity Definition

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1 Productive Capacity (Instrumental Good)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**[Productive Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#productive-capacity)** is one aspect of **[Shared-System Capacity](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. It names the lasting ability of sentients and shared systems to:
- support real participation, contribution, and skill-building; and
- turn time, attention, effort, coordination, materials, infrastructure, and energy into outcomes this Constitution actually requires.

It is an **instrumental good** — a means, not a trump value. Its job is to raise, sustain, and spread quality of life under the **Flourishing** aim, consistent with [Wellbeing](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim), [Dignity and Equal Moral Standing](core_05.055_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing), the Chapter Six Rights Floor, and the ecological and intergenerational limits of the **Continuity** aim under the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims).

## Draft translation (id)

**[Kapasitas Produktif](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#productive-capacity)** adalah salah satu aspek **[Kapasitas Sistem Bersama](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#shared-system-capacity)**. Istilah ini menamai kemampuan bertahan makhluk berkesadaran dan sistem bersama untuk:
- mendukung partisipasi, kontribusi, dan pengembangan keterampilan yang nyata; serta
- mengubah waktu, perhatian, upaya, koordinasi, bahan, infrastruktur, dan energi menjadi hasil yang benar-benar diwajibkan Konstitusi ini.

Ini adalah **kebaikan instrumental** — sarana, bukan nilai tertinggi. Tugasnya meningkatkan, mempertahankan, dan menyebarkan kualitas hidup di bawah tujuan **Kemajuan**, selaras dengan [Kesejahteraan](#3-foundational-objective-wellbeing-flourishing-aim), [Martabat dan Kedudukan Moral Setara](core_05.055_band_participation.md#dignity-and-equal-moral-standing), Lantai Hak Bab Enam, serta batas ekologis dan antargenerasi bagi tujuan **Kontinuitas** dalam [Dua Tujuan Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__productive_capacity_invalidation.md bytes=949 -->

## English source passage

Where productive-capacity claims rest on metrics that no longer prove real outcomes — including metrics that hide ecological damage, future harm, or concentration-driven loss — [§13.2.4 Proxy-Divergence Invalidation](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1324-proxy-divergence-invalidation) applies.

## Draft translation (id)

Jika klaim kapasitas produktif bertumpu pada metrik yang tidak lagi membuktikan hasil nyata—termasuk metrik yang menyembunyikan kerusakan ekologis, dampak buruk di masa depan, atau kerugian akibat pemusatan—maka [§13.2.4 Pembatalan karena Divergensi Proksi](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#1324-proxy-divergence-invalidation) berlaku.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — No Bypassing Principle-Weighing Rules

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

  - the other prohibited override paths in [§14 Prohibition on Absolute Override](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#14-prohibition-on-absolute-override), including shifting ecological, intergenerational, or distributional harm off the books that **Chapters Two through Four** require stay visible.

# Chapter One §9.1 — Productive Capacity in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1 Productive Capacity (Instrumental Good)

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: productive capacity is the "can we actually get things done?" aspect of shared-system capacity. Can sentients take part, learn, contribute, and turn effort and resources into results that make life better — and keep that ability over time? It is a tool for better living. What must be preserved, and what does not count, is [§9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count).*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: kapasitas produktif adalah aspek Kapasitas Sistem Bersama yang menanyakan, “bisakah kita benar-benar menyelesaikan sesuatu?” Dapatkah makhluk berkesadaran berpartisipasi, belajar, berkontribusi, dan mengubah upaya serta sumber daya menjadi hasil yang memperbaiki kehidupan — serta mempertahankan kemampuan itu dari waktu ke waktu? Ini adalah sarana untuk hidup lebih baik. Hal yang harus dipertahankan dan yang tidak dihitung dijelaskan dalam [§9.1.1 Mempertahankan, Memperluas, dan Hal yang Tidak Dihitung](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count).*

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — productive-capacity summary

## English source passage

*In plain terms: keep the ability to get things done, and grow it when that would waste less of everyone's time — but not by hoarding, faking the numbers, stripping rights, or dumping harm on others or the planet. Metrics that no longer prove real outcomes do not count.*

Metrik yang tidak lagi membuktikan hasil nyata tidak dihitung.*

- **Concentrating power or pushing costs onto others:**

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — Preserve Productive Capacity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Systems must preserve productive capacity and, where feasible, expand it when doing so would improve [Constitutional Efficiency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency) ([§9.2 Constitutional Efficiency](#92-constitutional-efficiency)).

## Draft translation (id)

Sistem wajib mempertahankan kapasitas produktif dan, jika memungkinkan, meningkatkannya apabila hal itu akan memperbaiki [Efisiensi Konstitusional](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#constitutional-efficiency) ([§9.2 Efisiensi Konstitusional](#92-constitutional-efficiency)).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — No Weakening Protections for Capacity

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- **Weakening protections:**
  - narrowing or delaying Chapter Six Rights Floors, including ecological preconditions under [Article I-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity) (*Environmental Preconditions and Ecological Integrity*);
  - weakening audit, contestability, or retrospective-review duties;

## Draft translation (id)

- **Melemahkan perlindungan:**
  - mempersempit atau menunda Batas Minimum Hak Bab Enam, termasuk prasyarat ekologis berdasarkan [Pasal I-A](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-i-a-environmental-preconditions-and-ecological-integrity) (*Prasyarat Lingkungan dan Integritas Ekologis*);
  - melemahkan kewajiban audit, ketergugatan, atau peninjauan retrospektif;

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9.1.1 — Proxy Measures That No Longer Track Outcomes

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count

## English source passage

- **Counting the wrong things:**
  - raw throughput, output volume, utilization, headcount, revenue, asset growth, market share, or similar proxies that no longer track real outcomes — including proxies that show "growth" while harm is exported to sentients, future generations, or the environment;

- **Menghitung hal yang keliru:**

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
