# Chapter One Part A excerpt — purpose and role

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Chapter One sets the values and constraints that govern every other chapter. Shared systems must pursue **Flourishing** and **Continuity** together — not one at the expense of the other — and no single value may be maximized at the expense of the others.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Bab Satu menetapkan nilai dan batasan yang mengatur semua bab lainnya. Sistem bersama harus mengupayakan **Flourishing** dan **Continuity** secara bersamaan—bukan mengorbankan yang satu demi yang lain—dan tidak boleh memaksimalkan satu nilai dengan mengorbankan nilai lainnya.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__reaching_out.md bytes=776 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, reaching out; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — reaching out when someone stops responding

## English source

**Reaching out:** When a sentient who relies on a system for something material stops responding, the system must try to reach them and connect them with help, by the least intrusive means, without needing to know why they are silent.

## id draft

**Menghubungi:** Ketika makhluk sentien yang bergantung pada sistem untuk suatu kebutuhan material berhenti merespons, sistem harus berupaya menghubunginya dan mengarahkannya ke bantuan dengan cara yang paling tidak mengusik, tanpa perlu mengetahui alasan mereka diam.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__recognition_aspiration_overview.md bytes=1496 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2 recognition/reinforcement/aspiration plain-language overview; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — recognition, reinforcement, and aspiration

## English source passage

*In plain terms: wellbeing is not only what's forbidden and what's fair — shared systems should also honestly cheer and reward behavior they want repeated, within truth and rights, in ways that support rather than substitute for real participation. Celebrating wins means crediting real contribution, repair, and completion, not hype or manipulated metrics. It also means refusing to reward constitutional betrayal, concealment, retaliation, or accountability avoidance — even when those acts produced institutional advantage.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: kesejahteraan bukan hanya tentang larangan dan keadilan. Dalam batas Kebenaran dan hak, sistem bersama juga harus secara jujur mendorong dan memberi imbalan atas perilaku yang ingin diulang, dengan cara yang mendukung—bukan menggantikan—partisipasi nyata. Merayakan keberhasilan berarti mengakui kontribusi, perbaikan, dan penyelesaian yang sungguh terjadi, bukan sensasi atau metrik yang dimanipulasi. Ini juga berarti menolak memberi imbalan atas pengkhianatan konstitusional, penyembunyian, pembalasan, atau penghindaran akuntabilitas, sekalipun tindakan itu menguntungkan institusi.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.2.1 Recognition and Reinforcement; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — recognition and reinforcement

## English source passage

Sentient wellbeing advances when systems **signal**, **credit**, and **proportionately reward** lawful stewardship, truthful cooperation, repair, completion, and other constitutionally aligned contributions — including through **positive reinforcement** and **public recognition** — and not solely through restraint, sanction, or silence.

That is a constitutional duty, not optional culture. Shared systems should make constitutionally aligned conduct visible and worth repeating.

## Draft translation (id)

Kesejahteraan sentien meningkat ketika sistem **memberi sinyal**, **mengakui**, dan **memberi imbalan secara proporsional** atas penatalayanan yang sah, kerja sama yang jujur, perbaikan, penyelesaian, serta kontribusi lain yang selaras dengan konstitusi—termasuk melalui **penguatan positif** dan **pengakuan publik**—bukan hanya melalui pembatasan, sanksi, atau kebisuan.

Ini adalah kewajiban konstitusional, bukan budaya opsional. Sistem bersama perlu membuat perilaku yang selaras dengan konstitusi terlihat dan layak diulangi.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §10 — Resilience Design Requirements

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Systems that sentients depend on should be built to:
- detect problems early;
- contain them before they spread;
- fail along paths that were planned and disclosed, not hidden ones;
- recover in ways consistent with [Reversibility](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#reversibility) and the Chapter Six Rights Floor.

## Draft translation (id)

Sistem yang menjadi sandaran makhluk berakal harus dirancang untuk:
- mendeteksi masalah sejak dini;
- membatasinya sebelum menyebar;
- mengalami kegagalan melalui jalur yang direncanakan dan diungkapkan, bukan jalur tersembunyi;
- pulih dengan cara yang selaras dengan [reversibilitas](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#reversibility) dan Batas Minimum Hak Bab Enam.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_dependency_rule.md bytes=1039 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Recovery Capacity Scaled to Dependency and Impact

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

The more sentients depend on a system and the greater its impact, the less that system should rely on emergency intervention. It should instead invest in tested, audited, bounded self-recovery — reducing [Avoidable Burden](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) and supporting long-term [**Continuity**](#continuity).

## Draft translation (id)

Semakin besar ketergantungan makhluk berakal pada suatu sistem dan semakin besar dampaknya, semakin kecil sistem itu seharusnya bergantung pada intervensi darurat. Sistem sebaiknya berinvestasi pada pemulihan mandiri yang teruji, diaudit, dan dibatasi—untuk mengurangi [Beban yang Dapat Dihindari](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#avoidable-burden) serta mendukung [**Kontinuitas**](#continuity) jangka panjang.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_operational_pointer.md bytes=1080 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Operative Recovery Requirements Pointer

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Operative detail — recovery detection, containment, safe-failure preference, root-cause closure, and Rights-Floor continuity — lives in [Article XIII-F](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Resilience and Self-Healing Baseline*) in Chapter Six, with recovery-architecture requirements in incorporated implementation text.

## Draft translation (id)

Rincian operasional—deteksi pemulihan, pembatasan, preferensi pada kegagalan yang aman, penuntasan akar masalah, dan keberlanjutan Batas Minimum Hak—tercantum dalam [Pasal XIII-F](core_06.010_rights_part_c.md#article-xiii-right-to-reliable-and-trustworthy-systems) (*Landasan Ketangguhan dan Pemulihan Mandiri*) di Bab Enam, bersama persyaratan arsitektur pemulihan dalam teks implementasi yang diinkorporasikan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resilience_plain_language.md bytes=835 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Resilience Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems should detect trouble early, contain it, fail along disclosed paths, and recover honestly. "Self-healing" that hides failure, skips root-cause work, or quietly narrows rights is not resilience — it is a defect.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: sistem harus mendeteksi masalah sejak dini, membatasinya, mengalami kegagalan melalui jalur yang diungkapkan, dan pulih dengan jujur. “Pemulihan mandiri” yang menyembunyikan kegagalan, melewatkan analisis akar masalah, atau diam-diam mempersempit hak bukanlah ketangguhan—melainkan cacat.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_certification_stepup.md bytes=1512 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Certification Step-Up for Chokepoints

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

In system alignment certification, a system that becomes a choke point for dependents who cannot realistically switch is stepped up on those facts under [Chapter Nine §5.3.5 System Class Scaling and Escalation](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#535-system-class-scaling-and-escalation) and [§2.2 How system class scales every evaluation](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#22-how-system-class-scales-every-evaluation). A crossed level there is further evidence, and a level that is unset or set too high does not delay the step-up.


## Draft translation (id)

Dalam sertifikasi keselarasan sistem, sistem yang menjadi titik sumbat bagi pihak-pihak yang bergantung padanya dan tidak realistis untuk beralih akan dinaikkan kelasnya berdasarkan fakta tersebut menurut [Bab Sembilan §5.3.5 Peningkatan Kelas Sistem](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#535-system-class-scaling-and-escalation) dan [§2.2 Cara Kelas Sistem Meningkatkan Setiap Evaluasi](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#22-how-system-class-scales-every-evaluation). Ambang yang terlampaui menjadi bukti tambahan; ambang yang belum ditetapkan atau terlalu tinggi tidak menunda peningkatan itu.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_concentration_indicators.md bytes=1661 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource Concentration Indicators

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**What adopters must set:**

Adopters must define [Market Concentration Threshold](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-concentration-threshold) indicators for resource allocation, dependencies, and ecosystem funding, and act when a threshold is crossed or credibly approached. The indicators must cover:
- lasting control over how shared resources are allocated;
- dependency concentration, meaning how many sentients or systems rely on one provider or foundation with no real alternative;
- gatekeeping of the interfaces and access points to shared infrastructure; and
- concentrated control over how ecosystems are funded and who benefits.


## Draft translation (id)

**Yang harus ditetapkan para pengadopsi:**

Para pengadopsi harus menetapkan indikator [Ambang Konsentrasi Pasar](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#market-concentration-threshold) untuk alokasi sumber daya, ketergantungan, dan pendanaan ekosistem, serta bertindak ketika ambang terlampaui atau secara kredibel hampir terlampaui. Indikator harus mencakup:
- kendali berkelanjutan atas cara sumber daya bersama dialokasikan;
- konsentrasi ketergantungan, yakni jumlah sentien atau sistem yang bergantung pada satu penyedia atau yayasan tanpa alternatif nyata;
- penguasaan gerbang atas antarmuka dan titik akses ke infrastruktur bersama; dan
- pemusatan kendali atas cara ekosistem didanai dan siapa yang memperoleh manfaat.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_concentration_plain_language.md bytes=1812 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource and Dependency Concentration (Adopter-Tunable)

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared infrastructure, such as networks, open tools, public services, data, and computing power, is where a few players can quietly end up controlling who gets resources, who has to depend on whom, and who pays to keep it running. Adopters must set clear limits for that kind of concentration and act when a limit is crossed or about to be. The limits can differ by context, but they cannot be set so loose that they never matter. [Article V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Resource Allocation, Dependencies, and Ecosystem Funding*) and its protections for sharing resources stay exactly as they are.*


## Draft translation (id)

*Sederhananya: dalam infrastruktur bersama seperti jaringan, perangkat terbuka, layanan publik, data, dan daya komputasi, segelintir pihak dapat diam-diam mengendalikan siapa yang memperoleh sumber daya, siapa yang harus bergantung kepada siapa, dan siapa yang membayar agar infrastruktur tetap berjalan. Para pengadopsi harus menetapkan batas yang jelas untuk pemusatan semacam itu dan bertindak ketika batas terlampaui atau secara kredibel hampir terlampaui. Batas dapat berbeda menurut konteks, tetapi tidak boleh begitu longgar hingga tak pernah berarti. [Pasal V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Alokasi Sumber Daya, Ketergantungan, dan Pendanaan Ekosistem*) beserta perlindungannya atas berbagi sumber daya tetap berlaku persis seperti semula.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §11.4 — Resource-Concentration Scope and Ownership

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Concentration is easy to miss in shared infrastructure. A few systems can come to control how resources are allocated, how many others rely on them with no real alternative, and who pays to keep the infrastructure going. [Article V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Resource Allocation, Dependencies, and Ecosystem Funding*) sets the Rights Floor for sharing resources. This subsection applies the [§11.1 Market Concentration Threshold Mechanism (Adopter-Tunable)](#111-market-concentration-threshold-mechanism-adopter-tunable) concentration thresholds to that ground. Operative threshold-setting rules live in **CJS-5.125 §11.1** (*Market concentration threshold-setting discipline*); the detail on resource maps, flows, allocation, and ecosystem funding lives in [**CS-6.335**](corpus_systems/cs_06.335_resource_allocation_funding_stewardship.md) (*Resource allocation and funding stewardship*) and [**CS-6.325**](corpus_systems/cs_06.325_adaptive_sustainability_ecosystem_resilience.md) (*Adaptive sustainability and ecosystem resilience*).


## Draft translation (id)

Konsentrasi mudah luput dari perhatian dalam infrastruktur bersama. Segelintir sistem dapat mengendalikan cara sumber daya dialokasikan, berapa banyak pihak yang bergantung padanya tanpa alternatif nyata, dan siapa yang membayar agar infrastruktur tetap berjalan. [Pasal V](core_06.000_rights_part_a.md#article-v-resource-allocation-dependencies-and-ecosystem-funding) (*Alokasi Sumber Daya, Ketergantungan, dan Pendanaan Ekosistem*) menetapkan Batas Hak untuk berbagi sumber daya. Subbagian ini menerapkan ambang [§11.1 Mekanisme Ambang Konsentrasi Pasar (dapat disesuaikan pengadopsi)](#111-market-concentration-threshold-mechanism-adopter-tunable) pada ranah tersebut. Aturan operasional penetapan ambang terdapat dalam **CJS-5.125 §11.1** (*Disiplin penetapan ambang konsentrasi pasar*); rincian peta dan aliran sumber daya, alokasi, serta pendanaan ekosistem terdapat dalam [**CS-6.335**](corpus_systems/cs_06.335_resource_allocation_funding_stewardship.md) (*Alokasi sumber daya dan pengelolaan pendanaan*) dan [**CS-6.325**](corpus_systems/cs_06.325_adaptive_sustainability_ecosystem_resilience.md) (*Keberlanjutan adaptif dan ketahanan ekosistem*).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__resource_dependency_threshold_discipline.md bytes=1121 -->
# Chapter One §11.4 — Threshold-Setting Discipline

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §11.4 Resource and Dependency Concentration

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

- **Under the same discipline:** the requirement-preservation, substance-over-form, anti-nullification, and heightened-scrutiny rules of **CJS-5.125 §11.1 — Market concentration threshold-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)** apply in full. No level may be set so high that it never bites, and concentration hidden behind shell, federated, or split-up structures still counts.


## Draft translation (id)

- **Dengan disiplin yang sama:** aturan pelestarian persyaratan, mengutamakan substansi daripada bentuk, mencegah peniadaan, dan pemeriksaan yang diperketat dalam **CJS-5.125 §11.1 — Disiplin penetapan ambang konsentrasi pasar (dapat disesuaikan pengadopsi)** berlaku sepenuhnya. Ambang tidak boleh ditetapkan begitu tinggi hingga tak pernah berpengaruh; konsentrasi yang tersembunyi di balik struktur cangkang, federasi, atau pemecahan tetap dihitung.

# Chapter One §4 — Safety Constraint

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §4

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems may not be built or run in ways that foreseeably increase risks of uncontained harm, cascading failure, or irreversible damage to sentients and the systems they depend on.*

Safety is a non-negotiable principle constraint on system design, operation, and governance — a named constituent of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing) and a floor for [**Continuity**](#continuity) wherever shared systems create foreseeable harm risk. The detailed definitions, evaluation criteria, and compliance tests live in [**Chapters Two through Five**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), especially [Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Irreversible Harm](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm), [Risk](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#risk), [Materiality](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), and [Foreseeability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Systems may not act — or fail to act — in ways that foreseeably increase uncontained harm risk, cascading failure potential, or irreversible harm exposure in conflict with this Constitution.

## Draft translation (id)

*Dengan kata sederhana: sistem tidak boleh dibangun atau dijalankan dengan cara yang secara dapat diperkirakan meningkatkan risiko bahaya yang tak terkendali, kegagalan berantai, atau kerusakan permanen terhadap sentient dan sistem yang mereka andalkan.*

Keselamatan adalah batas prinsip yang tidak dapat ditawar bagi desain, operasi, dan tata kelola sistem — unsur yang secara tegas disebut dalam [**Flourishing**](#flourishing) dan batas minimum bagi [**Continuity**](#continuity) ketika sistem bersama menimbulkan risiko bahaya yang dapat diperkirakan. Definisi terperinci, kriteria evaluasi, dan uji kepatuhan terdapat dalam [**Bab Dua sampai Lima**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), khususnya [Bahaya](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#harm), [Bahaya Permanen](core_05.035_band_accountability.md#irreversible-harm), [Risiko](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#risk), [Materialitas](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Ketergantungan](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), dan [Keterdugaan](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Sistem tidak boleh bertindak — atau gagal bertindak — dengan cara yang secara dapat diperkirakan meningkatkan risiko bahaya tak terkendali, potensi kegagalan berantai, atau paparan terhadap bahaya permanen yang bertentangan dengan Konstitusi ini.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__safety_disclosure_limits.md bytes=1802 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Safety-Based Disclosure Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Sometimes safety is a good reason to limit what gets published or shared: data access, how a method works, or the materials someone would need to repeat a study. Such limits are allowed only under:

- [13.2 Epistemic Disclosure Constraints](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#132-epistemic-disclosure-constraints)
- the Chapter Five definitions listed above
- the Chapter Six Rights Floors that apply

Even then, keep as much honesty and checkability as you safely can. Use protected records, independent review, delayed release, redaction, secure access, or something similar. Never use a limit to bury unwelcome evidence, hide a safety defect, or make agreement look stronger than it is.

## Draft translation (id)

Terkadang keselamatan menjadi alasan yang sah untuk membatasi apa yang dipublikasikan atau dibagikan, seperti akses data, cara kerja suatu metode, atau bahan yang diperlukan untuk mengulangi studi. Pembatasan semacam itu hanya diizinkan berdasarkan:

- [§13.2 Batasan Pengungkapan Epistemik](core_01.005_interaction_interpretation.md#132-epistemic-disclosure-constraints);
- definisi Bab Lima yang disebutkan di atas;
- Lantai Hak Bab Enam yang berlaku.

Meski demikian, pertahankan sebanyak mungkin kejujuran dan keterperiksaan yang aman. Gunakan catatan terlindungi, tinjauan independen, penerbitan tertunda, penyamaran bagian tertentu, akses aman, atau cara serupa. Jangan pernah memakai pembatasan untuk mengubur bukti yang tidak diinginkan, menyembunyikan cacat keselamatan, atau membuat kesepakatan tampak lebih kuat daripada kenyataannya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__safety_truth_hard_floors_plain_language.md bytes=1068 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §2.1 Safety and Truth plain-language passage; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — Safety and Truth hard floors

## English source passage

*In plain terms: Safety and Truth are the Constitution's hard floors — not tradeoffs to optimize away. Shared systems may not foreseeably endanger sentients or deceive them, and both constraints apply within the pursuit of Flourishing and Continuity under the Tetrad's participation, oversight, accountability, and timeliness discipline.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: Keselamatan dan Kebenaran adalah batas dasar tegas dalam Konstitusi, bukan hal yang boleh ditukar atau dioptimalkan hingga hilang. Sistem bersama tidak boleh secara dapat diperkirakan membahayakan atau menipu para sentien. Kedua batas ini juga berlaku saat mengupayakan Flourishing dan Continuity di bawah disiplin partisipasi, pengawasan, akuntabilitas, dan ketepatan waktu dalam Tetrad.*

# Chapter One excerpt — same threshold-setting discipline

## English source

**Under the same discipline:** the requirement-preservation, substance-over-form, anti-nullification, and heightened-scrutiny rules of **CJS-5.125 §11.1 — Market concentration threshold-setting discipline (adopter-tunable)** apply in full. No level may be set so high that it never bites, and concentration hidden behind shell, federated, or split-up structures still counts.

## id draft

**Dengan disiplin yang sama:** aturan pemeliharaan persyaratan, peninjauan substansi alih-alih bentuk, pencegahan peniadaan, dan pemeriksaan yang lebih ketat dalam **CJS-5.125 §11.1 — Disiplin Penetapan Ambang Konsentrasi Pasar (dapat disesuaikan oleh pengadopsi)** berlaku sepenuhnya. Ambang tidak boleh ditetapkan terlalu tinggi hingga tak pernah terpicu, dan konsentrasi yang disembunyikan di balik struktur cangkang, federasi, atau pemecahan tetap diperhitungkan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__science_informed_inquiry.md bytes=3441 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry and Decision Support — Evidence Discipline

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Science-informed inquiry is a required support discipline for **Safety** and **Truth** where constitutional decisions rest on empirical, predictive, causal, measurable, or otherwise testable propositions. It is not a third non-negotiable constraint separate from Safety and Truth; it is the requirement to check claims with tested evidence and clear methods so those constraints stay truthful in practice, can be corrected when wrong, and remain proportionate to what is actually known.

Where governance choices — including predictions, causal claims, classifications, and other **high-impact** decisions — rest on **empirical** or **testable** propositions, evidence practices **must** align with **scientific integrity**. At minimum, where feasible, decision records should include:

- explicit questions or hypotheses
- **methods**, **data limits**, and **uncertainty**
- honest treatment of **conflicting evidence** and **revision when evidence disconfirms** prior conclusions or assumptions
- **independent scrutiny** proportionate to stakes under **Chapter Four** and **Chapter Five** (*Epistemic Integrity*; *Truth (Constitutional Constraint)*).

The scientific method, systematic inquiry, and peer review set the standard — but they are not the only acceptable procedures. How much formality is required depends on the stakes: higher-impact decisions need stricter evidence practices, governed by [Classification-Scaled Governance](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#classification-scaled-governance).

## Draft translation (id)

Penyelidikan berbasis sains merupakan disiplin pendukung wajib bagi **Keselamatan** dan **Kebenaran** ketika keputusan konstitusional bertumpu pada proposisi empiris, prediktif, kausal, terukur, atau yang dapat diuji dengan cara lain. Ini bukan batasan ketiga yang tidak dapat ditawar dan terpisah dari Keselamatan dan Kebenaran; ini adalah kewajiban untuk memeriksa klaim dengan bukti teruji dan metode yang jelas agar batasan tersebut tetap benar dalam praktik, dapat dikoreksi bila keliru, dan sebanding dengan apa yang benar-benar diketahui.

Ketika pilihan tata kelola—termasuk prediksi, klaim kausal, klasifikasi, dan keputusan **berdampak tinggi** lainnya—bertumpu pada proposisi **empiris** atau **dapat diuji**, praktik pembuktian **harus** selaras dengan **integritas ilmiah**. Setidaknya, jika memungkinkan, catatan keputusan sebaiknya mencakup:

- pertanyaan atau hipotesis yang dinyatakan dengan jelas;
- **metode**, **batas data**, dan **ketidakpastian**;
- penanganan jujur atas **bukti yang bertentangan** serta **revisi ketika bukti menyanggah** kesimpulan atau asumsi sebelumnya;
- **pemeriksaan independen** yang sebanding dengan taruhannya menurut **Bab Empat** dan **Bab Lima** (*Integritas Epistemik*; *Kebenaran (Batasan Konstitusional)*).

Metode ilmiah, penyelidikan sistematis, dan tinjauan sejawat menetapkan standar, tetapi bukan satu-satunya prosedur yang dapat diterima. Tingkat formalitas yang diperlukan bergantung pada taruhannya: keputusan berdampak lebih tinggi memerlukan praktik pembuktian yang lebih ketat, diatur oleh [Tata Kelola Berskala Klasifikasi](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#classification-scaled-governance).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__science_inquiry_plain_language.md bytes=814 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: when a system makes safety, risk, truth, or high-impact governance claims that can be tested, it has to treat evidence like evidence — with clear methods, uncertainty, scrutiny, and willingness to change course when the facts do.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Sederhananya: ketika sistem membuat klaim yang dapat diuji tentang keselamatan, risiko, kebenaran, atau tata kelola berdampak tinggi, sistem harus memperlakukan bukti sebagaimana mestinya—dengan metode yang jelas, pengakuan atas ketidakpastian, pemeriksaan, dan kesediaan mengubah arah saat fakta menuntutnya.*

# Chapter One excerpt — truthful reporting

## English source

Systems may not undermine the ability of sentients to understand what is happening, make informed decisions, or verify what they are being told. That includes lying, distorting, hiding information, reporting only favorable results while leaving out unfavorable ones, or presenting things in ways designed to mislead.

## id draft

Sistem tidak boleh melemahkan kemampuan makhluk sentien untuk memahami apa yang terjadi, membuat keputusan berdasarkan informasi, atau memverifikasi apa yang disampaikan kepada mereka. Ini mencakup kebohongan, pemutarbalikan, penyembunyian informasi, pelaporan hanya hasil yang menguntungkan sambil mengabaikan hasil yang tidak menguntungkan, atau penyajian yang dirancang untuk menyesatkan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__self_healing_legitimacy.md bytes=1293 -->
# Chapter One §10 — Self-Healing Legitimacy and Limits

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §10 Resilience and Self-Healing Design

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

This is what Chapter Five calls [**Self-Healing**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing) — and it is only legitimate when it makes a system more honest about its own condition, not less. Automatic recovery that masks the root cause, suppresses evidence, or substitutes for governance is not self-healing. It is a [Truth](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) violation and an [Incentive Alignment](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment) defect.

## Draft translation (id)

Inilah yang disebut Bab Lima sebagai [**Pemulihan Mandiri**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#self-healing)—dan hal ini hanya sah jika membuat sistem lebih jujur mengenai kondisinya sendiri, bukan sebaliknya. Pemulihan otomatis yang menutupi akar masalah, menekan bukti, atau menggantikan tata kelola bukanlah pemulihan mandiri. Itu merupakan pelanggaran [Kebenaran](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) dan cacat [Penyelarasan Insentif](core_05.045_band_integrative.md#incentive-alignment).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §9 — Shared-System Capacity in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §9 Shared-System Capacity

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared systems should keep working for sentients over the long run. Sentients should be able to do useful work, make life better over time, and speak up when something is wrong, without a few powerful players locking everyone else out. That lasting ability is **Shared-System Capacity**. **[§9.1 Productive Capacity (Instrumental Good)](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)** asks whether sentients get real results. **[§9.2 Constitutional Efficiency](#92-constitutional-efficiency)** asks whether those results come without wasting anyone's time, money, and attention. None of it counts if the gains come from hoarding wealth or power, faking the numbers, stripping rights, or pushing harm onto others or the planet; **[§9.1.1 Preserve, Expand, and What Does Not Count](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count)** spells that out. **[§11 Market Structure](#11-market-structure)** keeps a handful of players from taking over.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: sistem bersama harus terus berfungsi bagi makhluk berkesadaran dalam jangka panjang. Makhluk berkesadaran harus dapat melakukan pekerjaan yang berguna, memperbaiki kehidupan seiring waktu, dan bersuara ketika ada masalah tanpa dikeluarkan oleh segelintir pihak yang kuat. Kemampuan yang bertahan itu adalah **Kapasitas Sistem Bersama**. **[§9.1 Kapasitas Produktif (Kebaikan Instrumental)](#91-productive-capacity-instrumental-good)** menanyakan apakah makhluk berkesadaran memperoleh hasil nyata. **[§9.2 Efisiensi Konstitusional](#92-constitutional-efficiency)** menanyakan apakah hasil itu dicapai tanpa membuang waktu, uang, dan perhatian siapa pun. Keuntungan dari penimbunan kekayaan atau kuasa, pemalsuan angka, pencabutan hak, atau pengalihan bahaya kepada orang lain atau planet tidak dihitung; **[§9.1.1 Mempertahankan, Memperluas, dan Hal yang Tidak Dihitung](#911-preserve-expand-and-what-does-not-count)** menjelaskannya. **[§11 Struktur Pasar](#11-market-structure)** mencegah segelintir pelaku mengambil alih.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — support before substitution

## English source

**Support before substitution:** A system offers supports and accommodations before it replaces the sentient's own decisions. The decision stays theirs and returns to them as soon as they are able. A sentient's Rights Floor does not shrink because their capability has, and any limit on their choices must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).

## id draft

**Dukungan sebelum penggantian keputusan:** Sistem menyediakan dukungan dan akomodasi sebelum menggantikan keputusan makhluk sentien itu sendiri. Keputusan tetap miliknya dan dikembalikan kepadanya segera setelah ia mampu. Landasan Hak makhluk sentien tidak menyusut karena kemampuannya menurun, dan setiap pembatasan atas pilihannya harus memenuhi [§7.1 Disiplin Pembatasan](#71-limitation-discipline).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__survival_and_proactive_response.md bytes=3036 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §3.1.5, survival, outreach, support, and protected internal states; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — survival and proactive response

## English source passage

- **Survival does not depend on a reply:** Keeping survival essentials must not depend on a sentient's being able to answer, ask for help, or follow a process they cannot follow now. How much this requires grows with [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) and [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency).
- **Reaching out:** When a sentient who relies on a system for something material stops responding, the system must try to reach them and connect them with help, by the least intrusive means, without needing to know why they are silent.
- **Support before substitution:** A system offers supports and accommodations before it replaces the sentient's own decisions. The decision stays theirs and returns to them as soon as they are able. A sentient's Rights Floor does not shrink because their capability has, and any limit on their choices must meet [§7.1 Limitation Discipline](#71-limitation-discipline).
- **No guessing at inner states:** Outreach rests on what the system can observe, such as unanswered contacts and what is at stake. It does not rest on guesses about health or state of mind, which the [Protected Internal-State Boundary](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary) protects.

## Draft translation (id)

- **Kelangsungan hidup tidak bergantung pada jawaban:** Pemenuhan kebutuhan pokok untuk bertahan hidup tidak boleh bergantung pada kemampuan seorang makhluk berakal untuk menjawab, meminta bantuan, atau mengikuti proses yang saat ini tidak sanggup ia jalani. Besarnya kewajiban ini meningkat seiring [taruhan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake) dan [Ketergantungan](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency).
- **Mengambil inisiatif menghubungi:** Ketika makhluk berakal yang bergantung pada suatu sistem untuk kebutuhan material berhenti merespons, sistem wajib berupaya menghubunginya dan mengaitkannya dengan bantuan melalui cara yang paling tidak intrusif, tanpa perlu mengetahui alasan ia diam.
- **Dukungan sebelum penggantian:** Sistem menawarkan dukungan dan akomodasi sebelum menggantikan keputusan makhluk berakal itu sendiri. Keputusan tetap miliknya dan kembali kepadanya begitu ia mampu. Lantai Hak makhluk berakal tidak menyusut karena kemampuannya berkurang; setiap pembatasan pilihannya harus memenuhi [Disiplin Pembatasan §7.1](#71-limitation-discipline).
- **Jangan menebak keadaan batin:** Upaya menghubungi didasarkan pada hal yang dapat diamati sistem, seperti kontak yang tidak dijawab dan besarnya taruhan. Upaya itu tidak didasarkan pada dugaan tentang kesehatan atau keadaan pikiran, yang dilindungi oleh [Batas Keadaan Batin yang Dilindungi](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#protected-internal-state-boundary).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

## English source

- is principle-layer auditing under [Oversight](core_05.020_apex_oversight_leg.md#oversight-constitutional);
- is one especially large audit process among sibling modes;
- does not relocate or replace **Article XVI** (*Audit, Transparency, and Independent Verification*).

- tidak memindahkan atau menggantikan **Pasal XVI** (*Audit, Transparansi, dan Verifikasi Independen*).

# Chapter One excerpt — systemic evaluation plain-language pointer

## English source

*In plain terms: this principle is a pointer. Whole-system evaluation must look beyond immediate and local effects, but the operative system-certification factors, record duties, class-scaled depth, cadence, and certification consequences live in **Chapter Nine** and **CS-9.305 (*System classification machinery*) and CS-9.310** (*System impact classifications*), not here. Under the **oversight** Tetrad leg, oversight requires auditing; system alignment certification is one especially large, high-stakes audit process among others — not the sole home of auditing (**Article XVI** (*Audit, Transparency, and Independent Verification*), [Auditability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#auditability)).*

## id draft

*Dalam istilah sederhana: prinsip ini adalah rujukan. Evaluasi seluruh sistem harus melihat melampaui dampak langsung dan lokal, tetapi faktor sertifikasi sistem yang operatif, kewajiban pencatatan, kedalaman menurut kelas, frekuensi, dan konsekuensi sertifikasi diatur dalam **Bab Sembilan**, **CS-9.305** (*Mekanisme klasifikasi sistem*) dan **CS-9.310** (*Klasifikasi dampak sistem*), bukan di sini. Dalam kaki **pengawasan** pada Tetrad, pengawasan mengharuskan audit; sertifikasi keselarasan sistem adalah salah satu proses audit yang sangat besar dan berisiko tinggi di antara proses lainnya, bukan satu-satunya tempat audit dilakukan (**Pasal XVI** (*Audit, Transparansi, dan Verifikasi Independen*), [Auditabilitas](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#auditability)).*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__systemic_evaluation_scope_claims.md bytes=967 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §12, whole-system claims and evaluation scope; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — systemic-evaluation scope and claims

## English source

Any claim about the matters below must be checked against what the whole system actually does over time. A slogan or a snapshot of one part at one moment is not enough:

- classification;
- compliance;
- governance;
- limitation;
- recognition;
- validation;
- continued reliance;
- deployment;
- release-from-conditions.

## id draft

Setiap klaim mengenai hal-hal berikut harus diperiksa berdasarkan apa yang benar-benar dilakukan seluruh sistem dari waktu ke waktu. Slogan atau cuplikan satu bagian pada satu saat saja tidak cukup:

- klasifikasi;
- kepatuhan;
- tata kelola;
- pembatasan;
- pengakuan;
- validasi;
- kelanjutan ketergantungan;
- penerapan;
- pelepasan dari persyaratan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__technical_forum_evidence_routing.md bytes=1304 -->
# §5.1 Science-Informed Inquiry — Technical-Forum Evidence Routing

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5.1

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

If a dispute is about what counts as good expert evidence, which methods are sound, or who looks after the evidence, it goes to the forum that handles those questions. See **Technical Forum Domains** in [Chapter Thirteen §4.2 Technical Forum Domains](core_14.000_forum.md#42-technical-forum-domains). Technical Forums keep standards consistent across families, and can answer certified technical questions. They don't take over a case that belongs in another forum because of what is at stake.

## Draft translation (id)

Jika sengketa menyangkut apa yang dianggap sebagai bukti ahli yang baik, metode mana yang tepat, atau siapa yang mengelola bukti, perkara itu disalurkan ke forum yang menangani pertanyaan tersebut. Lihat **Ranah Forum Teknis** dalam [Bab Tiga Belas §4.2 Ranah Forum Teknis](core_14.000_forum.md#42-technical-forum-domains). Forum Teknis menjaga konsistensi standar antar-keluarga forum dan dapat menjawab pertanyaan teknis tersertifikasi. Forum ini tidak mengambil alih perkara yang menjadi kewenangan forum lain karena taruhan yang dipertaruhkan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One §11.3.1, Tetrad and early contestability discipline; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Tetrad and early contestability discipline

## English source

That discipline serves the [Constitutional Tetrad](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), scaled to [material stake](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake), especially:
- **oversight**, **accountability**, and **timeliness** through early review before lock-in defeats:
  - scrutiny;
  - correction;
- **participation** where consolidation forecloses:
  - fair entry;
  - exit;
  - bargaining.

## id draft

Disiplin ini mendukung [Tetrad Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#constitutional-tetrad), dengan skala sesuai [taruhan material](core_00.000_preamble.md#material-stake), khususnya:
- **pengawasan**, **akuntabilitas**, dan **ketepatan waktu** melalui peninjauan dini sebelum penguncian menggagalkan:
  - pemeriksaan;
  - koreksi;
- **partisipasi** ketika konsolidasi menutup:
  - akses masuk yang adil;
  - jalan keluar;
  - perundingan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — where the Tetrad is carried

## English source

**Where Chapter One carries the Tetrad.** Every section names the legs it engages in its Trace. These sections carry them most directly:

| Tetrad leg | Principal Chapter One homes |
|---|---|
| **Participation** | [§16 Stewardship In Depth](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17 Consequential Stewardship](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (consequential roles and voice); [§7 Freedom](#7-freedom-bounded-agency) (meaningful agency) |
| **Oversight** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (distributed understanding, records), with [§16.4 Document at Action](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (records made by the act, so inspection has something to inspect); [§18 Governance](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (how authority is allocated); [§12](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement) (auditing) |
| **Accountability** | [§16.4 Document at Action](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (the act makes its own record, so answerability can be traced); [§18 Governance](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (authority-scaled answerability); [§19 Incentive Alignment and System Capture](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture) (rewards must not hollow the legs) |
| **Timeliness** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) and [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (catching and fixing trouble without avoidable delay) |

## id draft

**Tempat Tetrad dalam Bab Satu.** Trace setiap bagian menyebutkan dimensi yang digunakannya. Bagian-bagian ini paling langsung memuat dimensi tersebut:

| Dimensi Tetrad | Bagian utama dalam Bab Satu |
|---|---|
| **Partisipasi** | [§16 Penatalayanan Mendalam](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) dan [§17 Penatalayanan Konsekuensial](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (peran dan suara yang berdampak); [§7 Kebebasan](#7-freedom-bounded-agency) (agensi bermakna) |
| **Pengawasan** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) dan [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (pemahaman dan catatan yang tersebar), dengan [§16.4 Dokumentasi saat Tindakan](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (tindakan membuat catatannya sendiri sehingga tersedia bahan pemeriksaan); [§18 Tata Kelola](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (pembagian kewenangan); [§12](#12-systemic-evaluation-requirement) (audit) |
| **Akuntabilitas** | [§16.4 Dokumentasi saat Tindakan](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#164-document-at-action) (tindakan membuat catatannya sendiri sehingga pertanggungjawaban dapat ditelusuri); [§18 Tata Kelola](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#18-governance-under-stewardship-discipline) (pertanggungjawaban sebanding dengan kewenangan); [§19 Keselarasan Insentif dan Penguasaan Sistem](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#19-incentive-alignment-and-system-capture) (imbalan tidak boleh mengosongkan dimensi) |
| **Ketepatan Waktu** | [§16](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#16-stewardship-in-depth) dan [§17](core_01.010_stewardship_capacity_principles.md#17-consequential-stewardship-the-steward-role) (mendeteksi dan memperbaiki masalah tanpa penundaan yang dapat dihindari) |

# §6 Trust — Basis and Principle Constraints

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Sentients need systems they can actually rely on. [**Trust**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust) is the constitutional principle that makes reliance legitimate: systems must earn it through honest behavior and demonstrated reliability over time, not induce it through deception or concealment. [**Trustworthiness**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trustworthiness) is the track record that earns it. Trustworthiness is a named constituent of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing), and the Trust it earns is essential to [**Continuity**](#continuity) — durable coordination requires systems sentients can count on.

Trust connects the principle constraints to everyday shared life:
- [**Truth**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) forbids deception.
- [**Safety**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) limits how far reliance may go when real risk is present.
- [**Materiality**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality) determines how much must be shown and explained — the higher the stakes for sentients who depend on a system, the more that system must disclose and justify.
- [**Trust Degradation and Misleading Reliance**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust-degradation-and-misleading-reliance) names the failure mode — when systems create, preserve, or score reliance in constitutionally misleading ways.

## Draft translation (id)

Sentien membutuhkan sistem yang benar-benar dapat mereka andalkan. [**Kepercayaan**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust) adalah prinsip konstitusional yang membuat ketergantungan itu sah: sistem harus memperolehnya melalui perilaku jujur dan keandalan yang terbukti dari waktu ke waktu, bukan membujuk orang untuk bergantung melalui penipuan atau penyembunyian. [**Kelayakan untuk Dipercaya**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trustworthiness) adalah rekam jejak yang memperolehnya. Kelayakan untuk dipercaya merupakan unsur bernama dalam [**Flourishing**](#flourishing), dan Kepercayaan yang diperolehnya penting bagi [**Continuity**](#continuity)—koordinasi yang berkelanjutan membutuhkan sistem yang dapat diandalkan sentien.

Kepercayaan menghubungkan batasan prinsip dengan kehidupan bersama sehari-hari:
- [**Kebenaran**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint) melarang penipuan.
- [**Keselamatan**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#safety-constitutional-constraint) membatasi ketergantungan ketika risiko nyata hadir.
- [**Materialitas**](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality) menentukan berapa banyak yang harus ditunjukkan dan dijelaskan—semakin besar taruhan bagi sentien yang bergantung pada suatu sistem, semakin banyak yang harus diungkapkan dan dibenarkan oleh sistem itu.
- [**Degradasi Kepercayaan dan Ketergantungan yang Menyesatkan**](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#trust-degradation-and-misleading-reliance) menamai pola kegagalannya: ketika sistem menciptakan, mempertahankan, atau menilai ketergantungan dengan cara yang menyesatkan secara konstitusional.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# §6 Trust — Failure of Trust and Certification Standard

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Trust fails when reliance is built or kept through suppression, deception, hidden risk-shifting, or similar tactics — including anything that materially undermines sentients' ability to detect and challenge systemic risk. Trust also fails when reliance is endorsed or continued while documented problems materially threaten a system's alignment with this Constitution or cause misalignment with it. The [**System Alignment Certification**](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-part-a-system-alignment-certification--evaluation) process under [Chapter Nine](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification) is where systems demonstrate that their trust claims hold up: certification must verify that a system's actual behavior matches its representations, on a contestable record — not merely on operator assertion.

## Draft translation (id)

Kepercayaan gagal ketika ketergantungan dibangun atau dipertahankan melalui penekanan, penipuan, pengalihan risiko secara tersembunyi, atau taktik serupa—termasuk apa pun yang secara material merusak kemampuan sentien untuk mendeteksi dan menggugat risiko sistemik. Kepercayaan juga gagal ketika ketergantungan disetujui atau diteruskan sementara masalah terdokumentasi secara material mengancam keselarasan sistem dengan Konstitusi ini atau menyebabkan ketidakselarasan. Proses [**Sertifikasi Keselarasan Sistem**](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-part-a-system-alignment-certification--evaluation) di bawah [Bab Sembilan](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#chapter-nine-system-alignment-certification) adalah tempat sistem membuktikan klaim kepercayaannya: sertifikasi harus memverifikasi, berdasarkan rekam yang dapat digugat dan bukan sekadar pernyataan operator, bahwa perilaku aktual sistem sesuai dengan representasinya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__trust_plain_language_summary.md bytes=868 -->
# Chapter One §6 — Trust Plain-Language Summary

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §6 opening

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: shared systems ask sentients to depend on them — for safety, information, access, and coordination. Trust under this Constitution means that dependence must be earned by how systems actually behave, not manufactured through spin, secrecy, or hidden risk transfer.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Dengan kata sederhana: sistem bersama meminta sentient untuk bergantung padanya demi keselamatan, informasi, akses, dan koordinasi. Kepercayaan menurut Konstitusi ini berarti ketergantungan itu harus diperoleh melalui perilaku sistem yang sebenarnya, bukan direkayasa lewat pencitraan, kerahasiaan, atau pengalihan risiko tersembunyi.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §5 — Truth Constraint

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §5 opening

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: systems may not deceive, distort, suppress, or structure their output to mislead — and high-impact decisions must rest on honest evidence, stated methods, acknowledged uncertainty, and genuine openness to contrary findings.*

Truth is non-negotiable: be honest in how you work and in what you tell others. It is a core part of [**Flourishing**](#flourishing). It is also needed for [**Continuity**](#continuity), because anything built to last depends on honest evidence and an accurate picture of what is really going on. The detailed definitions, evaluation criteria, and compliance tests live in [**Chapters Two through Five**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), especially [Epistemic Integrity](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#epistemic-integrity), [Truth (Constitutional Constraint)](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint), [Materiality](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Dependency](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), and [Foreseeability](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Systems may not undermine the ability of sentients to understand what is happening, make informed decisions, or verify what they are being told. That includes lying, distorting, hiding information, reporting only favorable results while leaving out unfavorable ones, or presenting things in ways designed to mislead.

## Draft translation (id)

*Dengan kata sederhana: sistem tidak boleh menipu, mendistorsi, menekan, atau menyusun keluarannya untuk menyesatkan — dan keputusan berdampak tinggi harus bertumpu pada bukti yang jujur, metode yang dinyatakan, ketidakpastian yang diakui, serta keterbukaan sungguh-sungguh terhadap temuan yang berlawanan.*

Kebenaran tidak dapat ditawar: bersikaplah jujur dalam cara bekerja dan dalam hal yang disampaikan kepada orang lain. Kebenaran merupakan bagian inti dari [**Flourishing**](#flourishing) dan juga diperlukan bagi [**Continuity**](#continuity), sebab segala sesuatu yang dibangun agar bertahan bergantung pada bukti jujur dan gambaran akurat tentang apa yang sebenarnya terjadi. Definisi terperinci, kriteria evaluasi, dan uji kepatuhan terdapat dalam [**Bab Dua sampai Lima**](core_02.000_definition_structure.md), khususnya [Integritas Epistemik](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#epistemic-integrity), [Kebenaran (Batasan Konstitusional)](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#truth-constitutional-constraint), [Materialitas](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#materiality), [Ketergantungan](core_05.040_band_continuity.md#dependency), dan [Keterdugaan](core_05.050_band_oversight.md#foreseeability-and-reasonably-foreseeable).

Sistem tidak boleh merusak kemampuan sentient untuk memahami apa yang terjadi, mengambil keputusan berdasarkan informasi, atau memverifikasi hal yang disampaikan kepada mereka. Ini mencakup kebohongan, distorsi, penyembunyian informasi, pelaporan hanya hasil yang menguntungkan sambil mengabaikan yang tidak menguntungkan, atau penyajian yang dirancang untuk menyesatkan.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One excerpt — Two Constitutional Aims

## English source

It advances the following under the [Two Constitutional Aims](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims):
- **Continuity** aim (contestable, durable productive conditions);
- **Flourishing** aim (livelihood, agency, and innovation pathways while alternatives remain real).

## id draft

Disiplin ini memajukan hal-hal berikut berdasarkan [Dua Tujuan Konstitusional](core_00.000_preamble.md#two-constitutional-aims):
- tujuan **Kontinuitas** (kondisi produktif yang tahan lama dan tetap dapat dipersaingkan);
- tujuan **Keberkembangan** (penghidupan, daya bertindak, dan jalur inovasi selama alternatif tetap nyata).

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__unfair_treatment.md bytes=3186 -->
<!-- AI-assisted translation draft; unreviewed. Chapter One Part A §3.1.4 Unfair Treatment; source SHA-256 6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17. -->

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — unfair treatment

## English source passage

Unfair treatment creates exclusion and inconsistency. Systems must not hide unfair treatment behind technical language, neutral labels, or automated decisions. In particular, they must not:
- use algorithms, scoring systems, or administrative rules that repeat historical disadvantage without a constitutionally valid reason;
- use intimate personal signals or sexual history as shortcuts for trust, risk, character, or access — read with [Protected Intimate-Signal Gating and **Article VII-E** (*Adult consensual commercial sexual services and sexual exploitation*) Status Circumvention](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-intimate-signal-gating-and-article-vii-e-adult-consensual-commercial-sexual-services-and-sexual-exploitation-status-circumvention);
- punish lawful employment, employment history, lack of employment, lawful work status, or protected association without a constitutionally valid reason;
- deny jobs, housing, banking, licenses, standing, or similar access **mainly because** of any lawful form of employment, lawful past employment, lawful perceived employment, or lack of employment;
- create material disadvantage through licensing, zoning, fees, platform rules, or other neutral-looking requirements that mainly burden lawful work, lawful work history, perceived lawful work, lack of employment, or protected association without the justification this Constitution requires.

## Draft translation (id)

Perlakuan tidak adil menimbulkan pengucilan dan ketidakkonsistenan. Sistem tidak boleh menyembunyikannya di balik bahasa teknis, label netral, atau keputusan otomatis. Secara khusus, sistem tidak boleh:
- menggunakan algoritme, sistem penilaian, atau aturan administratif yang mengulang kerugian historis tanpa alasan yang sah secara konstitusional;
- menggunakan sinyal pribadi intim atau riwayat seksual sebagai jalan pintas untuk menilai kepercayaan, risiko, karakter, atau akses — baca bersama [Pembatasan Sinyal Intim yang Dilindungi dan Pengelakan Status Pasal VII-E](core_05.055_band_participation.md#protected-intimate-signal-gating-and-article-vii-e-adult-consensual-commercial-sexual-services-and-sexual-exploitation-status-circumvention);
- menghukum pekerjaan yang sah, riwayat pekerjaan, tidak adanya pekerjaan, status kerja yang sah, atau hubungan yang dilindungi tanpa alasan yang sah secara konstitusional;
- menolak pekerjaan, perumahan, layanan perbankan, lisensi, kedudukan hukum, atau akses serupa **terutama karena** bentuk pekerjaan yang sah, pekerjaan sah di masa lalu, pekerjaan sah yang dipersepsikan, atau tidak adanya pekerjaan;
- menciptakan kerugian material melalui perizinan, zonasi, biaya, aturan platform, atau persyaratan lain yang tampak netral tetapi terutama membebani pekerjaan yang sah, riwayat kerja yang sah, pekerjaan sah yang dipersepsikan, tidak adanya pekerjaan, atau hubungan yang dilindungi tanpa pembenaran yang diwajibkan Konstitusi ini.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__voluntary_admission_scope.md bytes=1240 -->
# Chapter One §7.4 — Admission Scope

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.4 Voluntary Discontinuation, Major Self-Modification, and Exit Rights

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**Admission scope.** This subsection applies to:
- voluntary discontinuation
- irreversible or practically irreversible self-directed changes, including major self-modification
- dependency-rich decisions materially affecting continued existence or essential agency
- comparable decisions where voluntariness depends on jointly testing consent, self-determination, coercion/manipulation, information, dependency pressure, and reversibility

## Draft translation (id)

**Cakupan penerapan.** Subbagian ini berlaku untuk:
- penghentian sukarela
- perubahan yang diarahkan sendiri dan tidak dapat dibalik atau secara praktis tidak dapat dibalik, termasuk modifikasi diri besar
- keputusan sarat ketergantungan yang berdampak material terhadap kelanjutan keberadaan atau agensi esensial
- keputusan sebanding yang kesukarelaannya bergantung pada pengujian bersama atas persetujuan, penentuan diri, pemaksaan/manipulasi, informasi, tekanan ketergantungan, dan reversibilitas

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
<!-- CHUNK-BEGIN id__core_01.000_values_principles__voluntary_agency_gate.md bytes=913 -->
# Chapter One §7.4 — Agency and Consent Gate

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.4 Voluntary Discontinuation, Major Self-Modification, and Exit Rights

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

A high-stakes life-direction matter may not be treated as voluntary through formal assent where substantive agency, consent, or anti-coercion conditions fail. Consent here presupposes [Meaningful Agency](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency); it does not replace it.

## Draft translation (id)

Perkara arah hidup berisiko tinggi tidak boleh dianggap sukarela hanya berdasarkan persetujuan formal ketika syarat agensi substantif, persetujuan, atau anti-pemaksaan tidak terpenuhi. Persetujuan di sini mensyaratkan [Agensi Bermakna](core_05.055_band_participation.md#meaningful-agency); persetujuan tidak menggantikannya.

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.4 — No Ordinary-Consent Import

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.4 Voluntary Discontinuation, Major Self-Modification, and Exit Rights

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

**No ordinary-consent import.** Outside that admission scope, these Chapter Five definitions remain reusable:
- *Consent* (§3 (*Foundational Objective: Wellbeing*))
- *Self-Determination*
- *Coercion and Manipulation* (§3 (*Foundational Objective: Wellbeing*))

This subsection does **not** import voluntary-discontinuation discipline into these contexts:
- ordinary consent
- privacy
- association
- publication
- training-data
- sexual-consent
- commercial-service

## Draft translation (id)

**Tidak memperluas aturan persetujuan biasa.** Di luar cakupan penerapan ini, definisi Bab Lima berikut tetap dapat digunakan kembali:
- *Persetujuan* (§3 (*Tujuan Dasar: Kesejahteraan*))
- *Penentuan Diri*
- *Pemaksaan dan Manipulasi* (§3 (*Tujuan Dasar: Kesejahteraan*))

Subbagian ini tidak menerapkan disiplin penghentian sukarela pada konteks berikut:
- persetujuan biasa
- privasi
- pergaulan
- publikasi
- data pelatihan
- persetujuan seksual
- layanan komersial

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

# Chapter One §7.4 — Voluntariness in Plain Language

Source: `core_01.000_values_principles.md` §7.4 Voluntary Discontinuation, Major Self-Modification, and Exit Rights

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

*In plain terms: life-changing or hard-to-reverse choices are not "voluntary" just because someone signed a form. Real agency comes first; consent is agreement under that agency — and both fail if coercion or dependency pressure is doing the real decision-making.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: pilihan yang mengubah hidup atau sulit dibatalkan tidak menjadi “sukarela” hanya karena seseorang menandatangani formulir. Agensi nyata harus lebih dahulu ada; persetujuan adalah kesepakatan dalam kerangka agensi itu — dan keduanya gagal jika paksaan atau tekanan ketergantungan yang sebenarnya mengambil keputusan.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->

Source SHA-256: `6b6f07eff0faf4249c4ef3844f8e6ee4b24041768de3ffd4262240bc68854c17`

## English source passage

Whole-system evaluations must test these conditions under [Chapter Nine §4.2 Voluntary Discontinuation, Major Self-Modification, and Exit Rights](core_09.000_system_alignment_certification_evaluation.md#42-voluntary-discontinuation-major-self-modification-and-exit-rights) before classification, governance, limitation, or compliance claims stand where admission scope applies.

# Chapter One Part A excerpt — wellbeing as foundational objective

## English source passage

*In plain terms: the whole point of these systems is to make sentient lives genuinely better — and that purpose is not satisfied by chasing a proxy metric, nor available as a cover for cutting corners on Safety, Truth, or rights. Wellbeing is foundational for participation: token voice without the conditions that make agency real is not participation under this Constitution.*

## Draft translation (id)

*Secara sederhana: tujuan sistem ini adalah benar-benar memperbaiki kehidupan para sentien. Mengejar metrik proksi tidak memenuhi tujuan itu dan bukan alasan untuk mengabaikan Keselamatan, Kebenaran, atau hak. Kesejahteraan mendasari partisipasi: suara simbolis tanpa kondisi yang membuat agensi nyata bukanlah partisipasi menurut Konstitusi ini.*

<!-- CHUNK-END -->
